# Contract function reference

Generated from the source-bound Solidity ABI and reviewed explanations. Update the notes and regenerate; do not edit this file by hand.

Covers **37 concrete project contracts, 826 functions, 37 constructors and 14 receive hooks**. Inherited ERC-721 and governance functions are included. Internal-only libraries, abstract bases and external dependency interfaces are not separate operator targets.

**Reads** use `view`/`pure`; on-chain calls still consume gas and can revert. **Writes** require a transaction and the stated permissions. A returned value from a write is available to simulation or another contract; a wallet receipt does not automatically contain that return value. Functions with no ABI outputs change state and/or emit events; no artificial return is invented.

`msg.sender`, payable `msg.value`, network/contract identity and operating-state guards are not ABI arguments. Payable does not mean arbitrary ETH is accepted. Unnamed parameters retain explicit zero-based positions. Tuple fields are expanded in ABI order; integer units and zero-address meaning depend on the reviewed operation, not the Solidity type alone.

The reference documents an undeployed candidate. It is not permission to broadcast, select providers or approve production. **Production NOT APPROVED.**

Missing function-purpose, caller or payable-value explanations: **0**. Top-level input positions without a dedicated explanation: **0**. Output positions without a dedicated explanation: **0**; their result meaning may instead be explained by the function purpose. These counts do not claim that every nested tuple field has a separate prose annotation. Missing semantics are not guessed from a Solidity type or vague name.

## ZZZAccessController

Direct governance-wallet authorization, fixed module binding/sealing, independent keeper/guardian roles and canonical lifecycle. No timelock, role scheduler or generic transaction executor.

[Source](../src/ZZZAccessController.sol). Category: Project contract.

### Deployment

| Function / mutability | Purpose | Who / conditions | Inputs | Outputs |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `constructor(address,address,address)`<br>nonpayable | Select governance, keeper and guardian; deploy fixed helper factories. Deployer holds temporary bootstrap only until seal. Emits the initial KeeperChanged and GuardianChanged grants. | Deployment only; three static nonzero address arguments. A governance wallet cannot be the controller itself. | `initialGovernance: address` — Reviewed governance wallet, typically a multisig; after sealing it calls configuration targets directly. Its own signatures/nonce authorize transactions.<br>`initialKeeper: address` — Nonzero initial bounded-maintenance operator; role changes separately from governance.<br>`initialGuardian: address` — Nonzero initial pause-only guardian; governance handover does not automatically rotate this role. | None in ABI. |

## ZZZActivationAssets

Fixed ETH/ZZZ-only contribution-source policy. ZZZ identity is selected once before sealing; same-token admission/pins remain governed. Pair accounting and NFT marketplace royalty currencies are separate.

[Source](../src/ZZZActivationAssets.sol). Category: Project contract.

### Deployment

| Function / mutability | Purpose | Who / conditions | Inputs | Outputs |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `constructor(address,address)`<br>nonpayable | Bind the immutable controller and Registry through the controller's fixed factory, without an arbitrary direct-construction bypass. | Only controller.activationAssetsFactory(); that factory's access() must equal controller. The fixed factory derives poolRegistry from its own create() caller. | `controller: address` — Existing AccessController contract; must be the factory's immutable controller.<br>`poolRegistry: address` — Nonzero Registry/caller address bound to this unique child by the factory. | None in ABI. |

| `SOURCE_POLICY_VERSION()`<br>view | Return the explicit fixed ETH/ZZZ input-policy profile version. | Read-only; any caller. | None in ABI. | `output0: uint256` — Version1: native ETH plus the one pre-seal ZZZ identity, with no third user contribution currency. |
| `access()`<br>view | Read the immutable controller binding. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: address` (contract IZZZAccess) — Controller address; not a treasury or royalty payout address. |
| `activePolicy(address)`<br>view | Read the current source-admission policy ID for an asset. | Anyone; read-only. | `input0: address` — Actual asset address; zero means native ETH. | `output0: bytes32` — Current append-only source-policy ID; zero means unset, not a healthy enabled source. |
| `isContributionAsset(address)`<br>view | Check permitted source identity only: native ETH or the configured ZZZ. Does not itself check enablement, runtime, price or precision. | Read-only; any caller. | `token: address` — Candidate original user-supplied currency; zero means native ETH. | `output0: bool` — True for native ETH or the fixed nonzero ZZZ identity, even when that identity's current source policy is disabled. value(nonzero) enforces the additional live policy checks. |
| `policies(bytes32)`<br>view | Read immutable source policy token, enabled state, code/review pins, decimals and admission provenance. | Anyone; read-only. | `input0: bytes32` — Immutable source-asset admission policy ID, obtained from activePolicy(asset) or policyAt(ordinal). | `token: address` — Actual token identity; zero denotes native ETH for an existing native policy.<br>`enabled: bool` — Whether this recorded source policy enables contributions; existence/identity/prices still require validation.<br>`expectedCodeHash: bytes32` — Expected token runtime hash, or the explicit native identity marker; not proxy implementation attestation.<br>`reviewHash: bytes32` — Recorded governance/admission review commitment; not proof of independent review.<br>`decimals: uint8` — Admitted raw token precision,0..18; zero is also an unset mapping default and must not imply a valid policy.<br>`poolAdmission: bool` — True if this record originated through initial authenticated pool-asset admission, rather than an explicit source-policy review. |

| `policyCount()`<br>view | Read the total immutable source-policy history length. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: uint256` — Number of append-only recorded source policies, including superseded/disabled records. |

After sealing: only current governance with canonicalState=0 and no preparation. No waiting period; ordinary pause alone does not block configuration. | `token: address` — Native ETH zero or fixed ZZZ when enabling; another historical token may only be disabled, not admitted as a third source.<br>`enabled: bool` — True validates and enables the permitted source identity; false stops future nonzero contributions without changing existing LP or claims.<br>`expectedCodeHash: bytes32` — Current reviewed token runtime hash, or NATIVE_ASSET_CODE_HASH for ETH. Disable paths record the supplied pin without querying a failing dependency.<br>`reviewHash: bytes32` — Nonzero commitment documenting this source-policy change. | `id: bytes32` — New immutable policy ID; prior history is preserved. |

Network gas is separate.<br>`reviewHash: bytes32` — Nonzero reviewHash provenance commitment; not a policy fingerprint, authorization or contract-verified review preimage. | None in ABI. |

| `recordCount(uint256)`<br>view | Read the exact captured record inventory length for a canonical domain, not NFT supply. | Anyone; read-only. | `input0: uint256` — Canonical domain slot0..5 (NFT,Vault,Membership,Rewards,Royalties,Fees). | `output0: uint256` — The exact captured record inventory length for a canonical domain, not NFT supply. |

| `settlementCursor()`<br>view | Read the next of six captured domains that must settle in order. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: uint8` — The next of six captured domains that must settle in order. |
| `stateRoot()`<br>view | Read the committed complete source/import state root, zero until complete export/import. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: bytes32` — The committed complete source/import state root, zero until complete export/import. |

### Write functions

| Function / mutability | Purpose | Who / conditions | Inputs | Outputs |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `abort()`<br>nonpayable | Begin pre-commit cancellation of a frozen handoff and permanently cancel the importer.<br>Does not immediately unlock source custody; funded legs must return first. | Selected source governance wallet directly, with the function's exact sealed/canonical phase and backing guards. | None in ABI. | None in ABI. |
| `acceptFunding(uint8,uint256)`<br>nonpayable | Acknowledge and classify one source-funded successor backing leg.<br>Cannot mark an unfunded or duplicate leg complete. | Only the authenticated source coordinator. | `slot: uint8` — Fixed domain/protection slot; use this component’s declared slot ordering.<br>`index: uint256` — Zero-based captured/list index; it is not itself an asset address or NFT ID. | None in ABI. |
| `activate()`<br>nonpayable | Validate all imported liabilities/backing and activate the sole successor in the source retirement transaction.<br>Not a public unpause or multisig-only override of missing backing. | Only the authenticated source coordinator after its terminal retirement. | None in ABI. | None in ABI. |
| `bindFrozen(bytes32)`<br>nonpayable | Bind an inert importer to the source’s exact frozen handoff identity and record counts. | Only the authenticated source coordinator. | `id: bytes32` — Exact authenticated frozen whole-collection handoff ID. | None in ABI. |
| `cancelImport()`<br>nonpayable | Mark the inert successor cancelled when its authenticated source aborts.<br>The cancelled importer cannot become a competing active collection. | Only source coordinator in its abort phase. | None in ABI. | None in ABI. |
| `commit()`<br>nonpayable | Atomically retire the source and activate its one fully funded compatible successor.<br>Requires matching deployment commitment, complete roots and every funded leg. No source resurrection after success. | Selected source governance wallet directly, with the function's exact sealed/canonical phase and backing guards. | None in ABI. | None in ABI. |
| `completeImport()`<br>nonpayable | Verify every imported count/root and mark the successor ready for backing.<br>Does not activate it or retire the source. | Permissionless after complete source-authenticated import. | None in ABI. | None in ABI. |
| `exportRows(uint8,uint256)`<br>nonpayable | Commit a bounded ordered batch of source records into the canonical snapshot root.<br>Maximum8 rows; uses on-chain cursors, not assumed operator progress. | Permissionless in the export phase. | `slot: uint8` — Fixed domain/protection slot; use this component’s declared slot ordering.<br>`count: uint256` — Number of consecutive rows to process, bounded by MAX_ROWS. | None in ABI. |
| `finishAbort()`<br>nonpayable | Reactivate the original source only after every sent leg is fully restored and validated.<br>Not available after committed retirement; no partial-backing override. | Selected source governance wallet directly, with the function's exact sealed/canonical phase and backing guards. | None in ABI. | None in ABI. |
| `freeze()`<br>nonpayable | Freeze ownership/accounting and atomically capture six source-domain cutoffs for the reviewed sealed importer.<br>Requires source/destination compatibility and parked classified backing. Permanently rejected after collective LP exit starts; terminal principal ledgers cannot be omitted by canonical replacement. | Selected source governance wallet directly, with the function's exact sealed/canonical phase and backing guards. | None in ABI. | None in ABI. |
| `fund(uint8,uint256)`<br>nonpayable | Transfer one exact classified source backing leg to the matching successor module.<br>Checks asset/amount and actual destination balance delta; no discretionary recipient. | Permissionless in funding phase after completed import. | `slot: uint8` — Fixed domain/protection slot; use this component’s declared slot ordering.<br>`index: uint256` — Zero-based captured/list index; it is not itself an asset address or NFT ID. | None in ABI. |
| `importRows(uint8,bytes[])`<br>nonpayable | Import source-authenticated ordered records into the inert successor.<br>Checks each row against the source’s exact export; cannot replace owners/shares/traits. Inputs: Slot and at most8 exact ABI record byte arrays in order. | Permissionless relay in importer phase. | `slot: uint8` — Fixed domain/protection slot; use this component’s declared slot ordering.<br>`records: bytes[]` — Ordered exact canonical record bytes; bounded batches preserve on-chain cursor/root. | None in ABI. |
| `prepareImport(address)`<br>nonpayable | Mark an unused unsealed successor as an inert same-chain importer for one source controller.<br>No ordinary mint/economic work until authenticated activation. Cross-chain transport is unsupported. | That deployment’s bootstrap before sealing. | `sourceController: address` — The one reviewed source controller for an inert compatible importer. | None in ABI. |
| `returnFunding(uint8,uint256)`<br>nonpayable | Send the cancelled importer’s exact classified leg back to its source. | Only authenticated source coordinator during abort. | `slot: uint8` — Fixed domain/protection slot; use this component’s declared slot ordering.<br>`index: uint256` — Zero-based captured/list index; it is not itself an asset address or NFT ID. | None in ABI. |
| `returnFunds(uint8,uint256)`<br>nonpayable | Recover one previously funded leg from a cancelled importer and restore source classification.<br>Verifies actual complete return; no timeout haircut. | Permissionless during source abort, exact funded/unreturned leg. | `slot: uint8` — Fixed domain/protection slot; use this component’s declared slot ordering.<br>`index: uint256` — Zero-based captured/list index; it is not itself an asset address or NFT ID. | None in ABI. |
| `settle(uint8)`<br>nonpayable | Settle exactly the next captured canonical domain before export/funding.<br>Six fixed-order domains; later receipts cannot silently enter the captured snapshot. | Permissionless with the exact phase/cursor. | `slot: uint8` — Fixed domain/protection slot; use this component’s declared slot ordering. | None in ABI. |

Marginal entrant deployment,maintenance retained-value and main/residual utilization are separate checks. | Actual current Vault MigrationBudget only; read-only integration helper, not a human-callable management operation. | `bucket: uint256` — Shared custody bucket ID,not NFT or physical LP ID.<br>`maxIdleBps: uint16` — Captured maxIdleBps,0..4000; denominator is deployed+idle at current validated NAV. | None in ABI. |

Round main floor up. Budget independently checks captured maintenance idle/loss. | `checkMaintenance(uint256,uint256,uint256,uint256,uint256,uint256,uint16,uint16,bytes32)`<br>view | Verify post-maintenance principal/idle using Budget starting USD vector,captured idle/loss limits and unchanged maintenance policy.<br>Requires positive deployment and retained value &gt;= beforeValue-floor(beforeValue*lossBps/10000). No repricing,reserve movement or skip of normal utilization checks. | Actual current Vault MigrationBudget only; read-only integration helper, not a human-callable management operation. | `bucket: uint256` — Shared custody bucket ID.<br>`beforeValue: uint256` — Positive starting attributed principal+released-compound USD18.<br>`price0: uint256` — Captured currency0 USD18 price.<br>`price1: uint256` — Captured currency1 USD18 price.<br>`scale0: uint256` — Captured currency0 decimal scale,10**decimals.<br>`scale1: uint256` — Captured currency1 decimal scale,10**decimals.<br>`lossBps: uint16` — Captured maintenance loss allowance in bps,0..1000; not gas.<br>`maxIdleBps: uint16` — Captured maintenance idle ceiling in bps,0..4000.<br>`expectedMaintenancePolicy: bytes32` — Exact ProtocolPolicy.maintenanceFingerprint() captured at start; later change rejects. | `deployed: uint256` — Actual aggregate deployed main+residual USD18 at captured prices.<br>`idle: uint256` — Actual attributed Vault idle USD18 at those same prices.<br>`poolId: bytes32` — Actual bucket poolId used by Budget identity checks. |
| `compoundReady(uint256)`<br>view | Evaluate the unchanged standalone compound policy for the actual fixed Budget. | Actual Vault-owned Budget only; stateless read. | `bucket: uint256` — Shared Vault custody bucket ID, not an individual NFT or physical LP token ID. | `output0: bool` — True only for active admitted LP after max(lastCompound,lastRebalance)+pool interval, yielding to raw rebalance/range selection. |
| `entryIdleLimit(uint256)`<br>view | Read only the active contribution context idle ceiling frozen before callbacks. | Actual current Vault MigrationBudget only; read-only integration helper, not a human-callable management operation. | `bucket: uint256` — Exact active pricing-context bucket ID. | `output0: uint16` — Captured entry maxIdleBps,0..4000; rejects absent/wrong-bucket/out-of-phase context. |
| `historicalValue(uint256)`<br>view | Value non-current-pair historical compound for incumbent backing; composition epoch zero returns zero. | Anyone; read-only. | `bucket: uint256` — Shared accounting bucket ID. | `value: uint256` — Conservative historical compound USD value scaled by 1e18; excludes current pair and does not fund an entrant execution floor. |

Aggregate actual both-leg principal; excludes unclassified gifts and reward/buyback backing.<br>`mark.value: uint256`<br>`mark.price0: uint256`<br>`mark.price1: uint256`<br>`mark.scale0: uint256`<br>`mark.scale1: uint256`<br>`mark.idle0: uint256`<br>`mark.idle1: uint256`<br>`mark.maxIdleBps: uint16`<br>`mark.maintenancePolicy: bytes32` |
| `nav(uint256)`<br>view | Read aggregate main/residual deployed NAV and attributed idle, never a redemption quote. | Anyone; read-only. | `bucket: uint256` — Shared accounting bucket ID. | `deployed: uint256` — Summed physical principal USD18.<br>`idle: uint256` — Attributed idle USD18; historical other-currency compound separate. |
| `oracle()`<br>view | Read the immutable Oracle binding. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: address` (contract IZZZOracle) — Oracle contract address. |
| `valueBucket(uint256,uint256,uint256)`<br>view | Value the given current-pair amounts using this bucket's frozen contribution prices when applicable, otherwise current Oracle values. | Anyone; read-only. | `bucket: uint256` — Shared accounting bucket ID.<br>`amount0: uint256` — Raw amount of the accounting pair currency0.<br>`amount1: uint256` — Raw amount of accounting pair currency1. | `output0: uint256` — Combined conservative USD value scaled by 1e18; no funds or shares move. |

| `canonicalState()`<br>view | Read the controller’s current canonical lifecycle state used by funding/forwarding guards. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: uint8` — The controller’s current canonical lifecycle state used by funding/forwarding guards. |
| `compoundBalance(uint256,address)`<br>view | Read one bucket/currency amount reserved solely for permanent LP compounding. | Anyone; read-only. | `input0: uint256` — Canonical shared bucket ID, not an individual NFT ID.<br>`input1: address` — Actual currency address; zero denotes native ETH in V2. | `output0: uint256` — One bucket/currency amount reserved solely for permanent LP compounding. |
| `dust(uint256,address)`<br>view | Read one bucket/currency’s unsplit fee remainder awaiting a valid allocation boundary. | Anyone; read-only. | `input0: uint256` — Canonical shared bucket ID, not an individual NFT ID.<br>`input1: address` — Actual currency address; zero denotes native ETH in V2. | `output0: uint256` — One bucket/currency’s unsplit fee remainder awaiting a valid allocation boundary. |

| Function / mutability | Purpose | Who / conditions | Inputs | Outputs |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `allocate(uint256,address,uint256)`<br>payable | Receive actual harvested bucket fees and apply the fixed 80/10/5/5 allocation.<br>**Native value:** Exactly amount when token is native zero; zero when token is ERC20 and the exact amount is pulled.<br>Uses raw units; residual sub-batch dust stays tracked. Does not include principal or mint revenue. | Only the fixed Vault in active canonical accounting. | `id: uint256` — Canonical shared bucket ID, not an individual NFT or physical LP NFT ID.<br>`token: address` — Actual currency address; zero denotes native ETH only in native-aware paths.<br>`amount: uint256` — Raw units of the actual specified currency; not its USD value. | None in ABI. |
| `canonicalBarrier()`<br>nonpayable | Reject a handoff capture from inside an allocation/reserve callback; no balance or entitlement is changed. | Only the fixed FeesCanonical companion, within its lifecycle guards. | None in ABI. | None in ABI. |
| `canonicalFlush(uint256,address)`<br>nonpayable | Settle captured home-bucket fee dust as part of canonical export preparation. | Only fixed FeesCanonical. | `id: uint256` — Canonical shared bucket ID, not an individual NFT or physical LP NFT ID.<br>`asset: address` — Actual currency address; zero denotes native ETH in native-aware paths. | None in ABI. |
| `canonicalSend(address,address,uint256)`<br>nonpayable | Transfer the exact asset amount during the fixed companion's classified handoff funding/abort procedure; no administrator sweep or arbitrary call. | Only the immutable FeesCanonical companion; its coordinator/lifecycle and exact-backing guards apply. | `asset: address` — Actual admitted reserve currency; address zero means native ETH.<br>`destination: address` — The exact destination chosen by the authenticated canonical funding/abort procedure.<br>`amount: uint256` — Exact raw units assigned to this backing transfer; not an amount fabricated from USD. | None in ABI. |
| `canonicalWrite(uint8,bytes)`<br>nonpayable | Import exact LP-only V3 asset or bucket rows under the fixed companion; old receiver/remote codecs are rejected.<br>No arbitrary storage or allocation rewrite. | Only fixed FeesCanonical. | `kind: uint8` — Explicit versioned record/proof type; unsupported values revert.<br>`record: bytes` — Exact versioned ABI-encoded canonical record authenticated against its source. | None in ABI. |
| `flushDust(uint256,address)`<br>nonpayable | Assign tracked sub-20-unit fee dust before a relevant membership/share boundary.<br>Preserves old entitlements and backing; not a free dust sweep. | Only sealed Vault or Membership. | `id: uint256` — Canonical shared bucket ID, not an individual NFT or physical LP NFT ID.<br>`token: address` — Actual currency address; zero denotes native ETH only in native-aware paths. | None in ABI. |
| `releaseBuyback(address,uint256)`<br>nonpayable | Release only the classified buyback reserve to fixed Buyback for checked execution.<br>Rejects zero/excess amount; does not let a keeper redirect revenue. | Only fixed Buyback. | `token: address` — Actual currency address; zero denotes native ETH only in native-aware paths.<br>`amount: uint256` — Raw units of the actual specified currency; not its USD value. | None in ABI. |
| `releaseCompound(uint256,address)`<br>nonpayable | Send a bucket’s designated compounding reserve back to Vault for reinvestment.<br>Cannot spend buyback or claimable balances. Returns: Raw reserve amount released; may be zero. | Only fixed authorized Vault. | `id: uint256` — Canonical shared bucket ID, not an individual NFT or physical LP NFT ID.<br>`token: address` — Actual currency address; zero denotes native ETH only in native-aware paths. | `amount: uint256` — Raw reserve amount released; may be zero. |

## ZZZLPBalancer

Separately deployed fixed-controller bounded native ETH/ZZZ quote planner: full mode maximizes sampled useful main value; hybrid minimizes sampled qualifying input. No custody or generic execution.

[Source](../src/ZZZLPBalancer.sol). Category: Project contract.

### Deployment

| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `constructor(address,address,address)`<br>nonpayable | Bind controller/core/state; create immutable stateless LPBalanceMath child. | Deployment only; Manager parent is controller-derived, not constructor caller. | `controller: address` — Fixed AccessController.<br>`core: address` — Reviewed v4 PoolManager matching eventual Manager.<br>`state: address` — Same-core StateView; parent constructor/admission verifies bindings. | None in ABI. |

| `MAX_EXECUTION_POOLS()`<br>view | Read immutable bounded candidate count. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: uint256` — Four reviewed execution pools maximum. |

### Write functions

| Function / mutability | Purpose | Who / conditions | Inputs | Outputs |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `configureExecutionPools(bytes32[],bytes32)`<br>nonpayable | Replace the complete bounded list of reviewed admitted execution pools. Empty clears swaps without price/core reads; this does not move LP or change its contribution destination.<br>Admission/list changes after sealing need no Solidity edit. Nonempty admission revalidates prices/fees/bindings. No automatic fallback, arbitrary token, router, calldata or recipient. | Before sealing: only the current bootstrap address, not a distinct governance wallet. After sealing: only current governance with canonicalState=0 and no preparation. No waiting period; ordinary pause alone does not block configuration. Additional guards: operating canonical lifecycle and no preparation, no terminal LP exit. | `pools: bytes32[]` — Zero through four unique native ETH/fixed-ZZZ static hookless v4 IDs already admitted and operations-enabled.<br>`reviewHash: bytes32` — Nonzero hash of independently reviewed configuration/dependency/route evidence. | None in ABI. |
| `configureSolver(uint8,uint32,bytes32)`<br>nonpayable | Change bounded quote search/gas policy, advancing executionRevision. Does not loosen price, caller or loss guards.<br>More search may cost more gas. Qualify exact settings/depth/provider gas; this is not an economic safety guarantee. | Before sealing: only the current bootstrap address, not a distinct governance wallet. After sealing: only current governance with canonicalState=0 and no preparation. No waiting period; ordinary pause alone does not block configuration. Additional guards: operating canonical lifecycle and no preparation, no terminal LP exit. | `iterations: uint8` — 4-12 attempts per reviewed venue; initial8.<br>`gasLimit: uint32` — 100000-300000gas per quote attempt; initial200000.<br>`reviewHash: bytes32` — Nonzero review-evidence commitment. | None in ABI. |
| `plan(bytes32,int24,int24,uint256,uint256)`<br>nonpayable | Compare no-swap and bounded quoted candidates using one independent price vector and actual target ticks; return the best sampled useful deployed value.<br>Full-mode bounded search at one fresh independent vector. Near-full no-swap deployment avoids dust-chasing. ## ZZZLPPositionExecutor

Immutable Manager-created typed LP executor for a Vault-owned main and at most one same-pool residual. Atomic collection/closure; no arbitrary action, recipient, sweep or governance custody path.

[Source](../src/ZZZLPPositionExecutor.sol). Category: Project contract.

### Deployment

| Function / mutability | Purpose | Who / conditions | Inputs | Outputs |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `constructor(address,address,address,address,address,address)`<br>nonpayable | Bind creating Manager and exact v4 dependencies. | Normally created by reviewed Manager constructor; liquidityManager is immutable constructor msg.sender, unlike controller-derived Budget/Balancer parents. | `controller: address` — Fixed AccessController.<br>`positionManager_: address` — Same reviewed PositionManager as parent.<br>`stateView_: address` — Same reviewed StateView.<br>`poolManager_: address` — Same reviewed PoolManager.<br>`permit2_: address` — Same Permit2 used by PositionManager.<br>`balancer_: address` — Parent's reviewed Balancer. | None in ABI. |

Exact runtime/parent bindings still require review. |

Vault must harvest fees first; governance does not call this lower-level function directly. No arbitrary LP ID, recipient, swap or sweep is admitted. | Only its immutable current sealed Manager while globally paused; nonreentrant, pause rechecked after callbacks. | `poolId: bytes32` — Exact admitted native ETH/fixed-ZZZ v4 PoolKey hash; not a pool contract address.<br>`main: uint256` — Actual registered main Uniswap LP NFT ID. The operation resolves its sole associated residual.<br>`deadline: uint256` — Unix seconds: block.timestamp &lt;= deadline &lt;= block.timestamp+600 at execution. | None in ABI. |
| `collect(bytes32,uint256,uint256)`<br>nonpayable | Collect main/residual fees atomically without removing principal; exact receipts go only to Vault.<br>Vault allocates80/10/5/5 after actual bucket receipt; collection does not itself compound,buy back or claim. | Only sealed current immutable Manager; nonreentrant. | `poolId: bytes32` — Exact admitted native ETH/fixed-ZZZ v4 PoolKey hash, not a pool contract address.<br>`id: uint256` — Current registered main Uniswap LP NFT ID; resolve the optional residual separately.<br>`deadline: uint256` — Unix seconds: block.timestamp &lt;= deadline &lt;= block.timestamp+600 at execution. | None in ABI. |
| `increase(bytes32,uint256,uint256,uint256,uint128,uint256)`<br>payable | Grow authenticated main at unchanged bounds; residual untouched.<br>**Native value:** Exactly amount0 ETH forwarded by Manager.<br>Check exact main L delta and preserve unspent/prefunded balances and residual. | Only sealed current immutable Manager; nonreentrant. | `poolId: bytes32` — Exact admitted native ETH/fixed-ZZZ v4 PoolKey hash, not a pool contract address.<br>`id: uint256` — Current registered main Uniswap LP NFT ID; resolve the optional residual separately.<br>`amount0: uint256` — Exact available ETH wei.<br>`amount1: uint256` — Exact available ZZZ raw units, approved to this child.<br>`minimumLiquidity: uint128` — Minimum added MAIN liquidity units.<br>`deadline: uint256` — Unix seconds: block.timestamp &lt;= deadline &lt;= block.timestamp+600 at execution. | `spent0: uint256` — Actual ETH wei spent.<br>`spent1: uint256` — Actual ZZZ raw units spent. |
| `mint(bytes32,int24,int24,uint256,uint256,uint128,uint256)`<br>payable | Mint Vault-owned main and optional residual from only the supplied attributed budget.<br>**Native value:** Exactly amount0 ETH forwarded by Manager.<br>Residual only during enabled normal replacement, never first activation/compound/emergency. Ordinary hybrid main must actually be in-range at mint. Verify exact debts/final spot/key/L and return unused funds only to Vault. Both NFTs remain Vault-owned; small rounding leftovers stay attributed idle. Hybrid main physical mark and lower&lt;=tick&lt;upper are rechecked after all external execution/refund callbacks; a callback that leaves it inactive atomically reverts. Normal whole-position replacement requires an actually active main range before and after all mint/refund callbacks in both single and hybrid modes. This does not prohibit already-authorized initial/growth out-of-range contributions or reinterpret the explicit paused emergency manifest. | Only immutable creating Manager while sealed current LIQUIDITY module; nonreentrant. | `poolId: bytes32` — Exact admitted native ETH/fixed-ZZZ v4 PoolKey hash, not a pool contract address.<br>`lower: int24` — Reviewed main lower tick.<br>`upper: int24` — Reviewed main upper tick.<br>`amount0: uint256` — Exact available ETH wei.<br>`amount1: uint256` — Exact available ZZZ raw units, approved by Vault to this child.<br>`minimumLiquidity: uint128` — Minimum MAIN liquidity units, not aggregate L/USD.<br>`deadline: uint256` — Unix seconds: block.timestamp &lt;= deadline &lt;= block.timestamp+600 at execution. | `tokenId: uint256` — New main LP ID; residual from secondaryId(main).<br>`spent0: uint256` — Actual ETH wei spent across both legs.<br>`spent1: uint256` — Actual ZZZ raw units spent across both legs. |

Forced/prefunded balances are separate from measured operation credit. | Only currently armed nativeSender; otherwise revert. | None in ABI. | None in ABI. |

## ZZZLPRewardConverter

Fixed LP rewards-ledger-only executor converting earned ERC20 balances into native ETH. Shares the abstract ZZZRewardConverter implementation, but has its own address, immutable ledger, route policies and storage; no shared custody or new authority.

[Source](../src/ZZZLPRewardConverter.sol). Category: Project contract.

### Deployment

| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `constructor(address,address,address)`<br>nonpayable | Deploy and bind Fixed LP-ledger-only executor converting already earned ERC20 rewards into native ETH without changing NFT attribution. | Deployment only; required creator/controller/parent checks apply before this instance can be bound/sealed. | `controller: address` — Fixed AccessController address for this deployment’s roles and lifecycle.<br>`rewards: address` — Immutable LP Rewards ledger alone permitted to use its LP converter.<br>`wrappedETH: address` — Optional immutable WETH address for explicit unwrap/v3 paths; zero is allowed for native-only deployment. | None in ABI. |

Inputs: zeroForOne selects ordered currency0→1; raw input/minimumOut and Unix deadline bound execution. Returns: Actual raw output amount. A zero minimum rejects. Full policy context is captured before input pull and rechecked after output. Returns: ABI-encoded measured swap output. | Only bound PoolManager with the matching one-time pending payload hash. | `data: bytes` — Exact pending ABI payload; callback authentication binds its hash. | `output0: bytes` — ABI-encoded measured swap output. |

## ZZZMigration

Governed same-pair movement/local parking while operating, or irreversible collective ETH+ZZZ principal settlement to current NFT holders. No external custody address, consolidation exit or sweep. Separate manual paused-governance emergency rebalance retains explicit checks; disabled financial guards can permit near-total economic loss without changing recipients or NFT shares.

Exact runtime/parent bindings still require review. |
| `access()`<br>view | Read the fixed controller that supplies module, governance, keeper and lifecycle authorization. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: address` (contract IZZZAccess) — Bound AccessController address; reading it grants no authority. |
| `activePlan(uint256)`<br>pure | Read the reserved compatibility value used by pre-exit canonical/shared-accounting checks. | Anyone; read-only. | `input0: uint256` — Stable shared bucket ID, not an individual NFT ID, mint ordinal or Uniswap LP token ID. | `output0: bytes32` — Always bytes32(0): this implementation has no external custody plans. Use exitPhase and Access.lpExitStarted for holder exit. |
| `allocationCursor()`<br>view | Read the next one-based captured mint ordinal to apportion. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: uint256` — 0 before allocation;1 at its start;exitMintedCount+1 after complete inventory verification. This is not a token ID. |
| `bucketSettlement(uint256)`<br>view | Read one bucket's frozen denominator, actual returned amounts and cumulative allocation progress. | Anyone; read-only. | `input0: uint256` — Stable shared bucket ID, not an individual NFT ID, mint ordinal or Uniswap LP token ID. | `totalShares: uint256` — Frozen total bucket shares. Zero means the bucket was not captured.<br>`ethAmount: uint256` — Actual native ETH returned for this bucket, in wei.<br>`zzzAmount: uint256` — Actual fixed-ZZZ returned for this bucket, in raw units.<br>`allocatedShares: uint256` — Sum of NFT shares already apportioned for this bucket.<br>`allocatedETH: uint256` — Cumulative ETH assigned to NFTs, in wei.<br>`allocatedZZZ: uint256` — Cumulative ZZZ assigned to NFTs, in raw units.<br>`closed: bool` — True only after exact bucket closing and funding succeeded. |
| `claimablePrincipal(uint256)`<br>view | Read remaining ETH+ZZZ principal entitlement after complete settlement allocation.<br>Reverts NotReady before phase3 or for an unprocessed/nonexistent NFT; never guess an unallocated amount as zero. A processed dormant/zero-output NFT legitimately has zero principal. | Anyone; read-only. | `id: uint256` — Actual canonical PoPL NFT ID; not the display number, mint ordinal or shared bucket ID. | `ethAmount: uint256` — Unpaid native ETH wei, or zero once paid.<br>`zzzAmount: uint256` — Unpaid fixed-ZZZ raw units, or zero once paid. |
| `currentZZZ()`<br>view | Read the one fixed ZZZ identity whose actual raw principal is returned alongside native ETH. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: address` — Bootstrap-bound ZZZ ERC20, matching contribution-source admission and immutable Buyback; never a custody beneficiary. |
| `custodyEpoch(uint256)`<br>view | Read the bucket custody epoch bound by parking and residual-close manifests. | Anyone; read-only. | `input0: uint256` — Stable shared bucket ID, not an individual NFT ID, mint ordinal or Uniswap LP token ID. | `output0: uint64` — Epoch advances on terminal bucket closure or successful residual-only close; no external custody departure plan exists. |
| `emergencyCustodyHash(uint256)`<br>view | Read the exact physical/accounting identity to review for one emergency operation.<br>Binds both actual liquidity amounts as well as stable IDs/key/bounds. A compound invalidates stale review even if main ID/ticks remain; this differs from sampled identity. | Anyone; read-only. | `bucket: uint256` — Shared Vault custody bucket ID, not an individual NFT or physical LP token ID. | `output0: bytes32` — keccak256(chainid,Vault,bucket,full Position,total shares). Contributions/park/move/idle/share changes invalidate it; price changes alone do not. |
| `emergencyFingerprint()`<br>view | Read the stored emergency manifest, current governance and monotonic Registry controls, adding source/quality pins when retained. | Only the actual Vault-owned Budget during a validated emergency context. | None in ABI. | `result: bytes32` — Context hash; invalid context rejects. Retained sampled policy/raw record is additionally pinned by Budget. |
| `emergencyRequest()`<br>view | Inspect the temporary emergency request; zero fields outside its atomic call. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: (uint256,bytes32,uint8,int24,int24,bytes32,bool,uint128,uint128,uint128,uint256,bytes32)` (struct EmergencyRebalance) — EmergencyRebalance in exact ABI order: bucket(shared custody ID); custodyHash(current chain/Vault/full Position/total-share review hash); checks(retained bits1=raw trigger/cooldown,2=price agreement,4=financial loss/idle/quality,8=operations flag,16=sampled confirmation;0..31); lower/upper(raw spaced physical ticks, lower&lt;upper); swapPool(admitted native ETH/ZZZ venue or zero for no swap); zeroForOne(true sells ETH); amountIn(exact raw swap input, zero=no swap); minimumOut(positive raw output if swapping, zero without swap); minimumLiquidity(positive raw LP floor); deadline(now..now+600); reviewHash(nonzero exact review commitment). No swap requires swapPool and minimumOut zero; amounts fit positive signed-int128 deltas. Disabled financial checks can lose almost all principal.<br>`output0.bucket: uint256`<br>`output0.custodyHash: bytes32`<br>`output0.checks: uint8`<br>`output0.lower: int24`<br>`output0.upper: int24`<br>`output0.swapPool: bytes32`<br>`output0.zeroForOne: bool`<br>`output0.amountIn: uint128`<br>`output0.minimumOut: uint128`<br>`output0.minimumLiquidity: uint128`<br>`output0.deadline: uint256`<br>`output0.reviewHash: bytes32` |
| `emergencyTarget()`<br>view | Inspect explicit raw physical target ticks from the temporary request. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: int24` — Lower raw tick; zero outside a request.<br>`output1: int24` — Upper raw tick; zero outside a request. |
| `exitMintedCount()`<br>view | Read the immutable mint-inventory length captured when exit started. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: uint256` — Captured NFT count, at most1000, including dormant/unrevealed NFTs. New mints are permanently stopped. |
| `exitPhase()`<br>view | Read the collective exit workflow phase, separate from canonical lifecycle and ordinary pause. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: uint8` (enum ZZZMigration.ExitPhase) — 0Operating;1Closing funded buckets;2Allocating immutable mint inventory;3Ready for holder payouts. No cancellation or return to Operating. |
| `exitReviewHash()`<br>view | Read governance's nonzero review commitment for the irreversible collective exit. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: bytes32` — Committed review hash; zero before initiation. The hash itself does not perform a safety review. |
| `outstandingCount()`<br>pure | Read the reserved count for removed external-custody plans. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: uint256` — Always0. This does not indicate that holder principal is paid; inspect remainingETH/remainingZZZ and per-NFT paid markers. |
| `parkingNonce(uint256)`<br>view | Read the shared anti-replay nonce for parking and residual-only close reviews. | Anyone; read-only. | `input0: uint256` — Stable shared bucket ID, not an individual NFT ID, mint ordinal or Uniswap LP token ID. | `output0: uint64` — Current nonce; advances on successful parking, residual-only close or explicit invalidateParking. Read again before reviewing another operation. |
| `principalOf(uint256)`<br>view | Read the NFT's original principal allocation and processing/payment markers.<br>These amounts are principal, not LP income, royalty income, current attached LP or cumulative earnings. Unpaid rights follow the NFT. | Anyone; read-only. | `input0: uint256` — Actual canonical PoPL NFT ID; not the display number, mint ordinal or shared bucket ID. | `ethAmount: uint256` — Original allocated native ETH in wei; retained as history after payment.<br>`zzzAmount: uint256` — Original allocated fixed-ZZZ raw units; retained after payment.<br>`allocated: bool` — Whether its captured mint ordinal was processed; false is not proof of zero entitlement.<br>`paid: bool` — Whether this NFT's nonzero allocation has already been paid once. |
| `remainingBuckets()`<br>view | Count snapshotted funded buckets still awaiting an exact principal close. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: uint256` — Remaining closures; zero once every funded bucket closed. It is not the number of unpaid NFTs. |
| `remainingETH()`<br>view | Read aggregate classified native principal not yet paid to NFT holders. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: uint256` — Wei backed by settlement custody; excludes unsolicited prefunds and separate fee/royalty ledgers. |
| `remainingZZZ()`<br>view | Read aggregate classified fixed-ZZZ principal not yet paid to NFT holders. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: uint256` — Fixed-ZZZ raw units backed by settlement custody; excludes unsolicited prefunds and separate ledgers. |
| `settlementBucketAt(uint256)`<br>view | Enumerate captured funded bucket IDs. | Anyone; read-only. | `index: uint256` — Zero-based index strictly below settlementBucketCount. | `output0: uint256` — Stable shared bucket ID, not an individual NFT ID, mint ordinal or Uniswap LP token ID. |
| `settlementBucketCount()`<br>view | Read the number of funded buckets captured for this collective exit. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: uint256` — At most Vault.MAX_BUCKETS=4. Empty reservations are omitted. |
| `validation()`<br>view | Locate the immutable read-only local parking validator. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: address` (contract ZZZCustodyValidation) — ZZZCustodyValidation deployed by this Migration; has no principal custody or recipient-selection authority. |
| `zzzCodeHash()`<br>view | Read the runtime-code identity reviewed when the fixed ZZZ token was bootstrap-bound. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: bytes32` — Stored reviewed runtime hash; it is not a proof of token behavior or proxy/admin independence. |

### Write functions

| Function / mutability | Purpose | Who / conditions | Inputs | Outputs |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `allocatePrincipal(uint256)`<br>nonpayable | Advance deterministic proportional principal apportionment over bounded captured mint ordinals.<br>Cumulative floor differences allocate every raw unit in immutable mint order, by preserved bucket shares. Final checks prove all denominators/output totals. Emits ERC-4906 updates for funded NFT IDs. No payout occurs until all buckets/inventory pass. | Anyone in canonical operating state, not preparing; requires Allocating phase. | `maximum: uint256` — Maximum mint ordinals to process in this call,1..32; includes dormant ordinals. | None in ABI. |
| `beginCollectiveExit(bytes32)`<br>nonpayable | Validate every funded bucket and captured mint inventory, then irreversibly start collective principal return.<br>Accepts only native ETH/fixed-ZZZ funded buckets in Active/Parked state. Does not close LP or pay anyone yet. The Vault barrier sets lpExitStarted; no cancellation/reopening is available. Does not require NFT reveal completion, prices or swaps. | Current governance through controller; sealed, paused, canonical operating and not preparing. | `reviewHash: bytes32` — Nonzero review commitment covering irreversible shutdown and the operator's close/distribution plan. | None in ABI. |
| `claimPrincipal(uint256,address)`<br>nonpayable | Recover an unpaid nonzero principal allocation to an owner-chosen recipient.<br>Optional fallback for rejecting/expensive automatic receivers. Pays the same one-time ETH+ZZZ allocation; does not claim LP fees or royalties, spend another NFT backing or reopen LP. Ordinary pause does not block this fallback. | Actual current NFT owner only, not ERC721 approvals; Ready phase and canonical operating lifecycle, not preparing. | `id: uint256` — Actual canonical PoPL NFT ID; not the display number, mint ordinal or shared bucket ID.<br>`recipient: address` — Nonzero recipient chosen by the current owner; cannot be this settlement contract. | None in ABI. |
| `closeBucket(uint256,uint256,uint256,uint256)`<br>nonpayable | Final-harvest and close one captured bucket into exact ETH+ZZZ settlement backing.<br>Each captured bucket closes once. Excludes earned rewards, royalties, buyback reserves and prefunds. No swap/USD-oracle or aggregate USD-loss promise. Last closure starts ordinal allocation. Broken venue/token or failed minima revert that close. | Current governance; terminal exit in Closing, paused and canonical operating. | `bucket: uint256` — Stable shared bucket ID, not an individual NFT ID, mint ordinal or Uniswap LP token ID.<br>`minimumETH: uint256` — Minimum total returned native ETH wei, including attributed idle and released compound. Zero is deliberate raw acceptance.<br>`minimumZZZ: uint256` — Minimum total returned fixed-ZZZ raw units, including attributed idle and released compound. Zero is deliberate raw acceptance.<br>`deadline: uint256` — Inclusive Unix timestamp in seconds, no earlier than this block and no more than ten minutes ahead. | None in ABI. |
| `closeResidual((uint256,bytes32,uint256,uint256,uint256,uint64,uint64,address,address,uint128,uint128,uint256,bytes32,bytes32))`<br>nonpayable | Manually remove only the reviewed residual LP into Vault idle while preserving the main and all NFT rights.<br>Shares the parking review nonce/custody epoch, both advanced on success. Harvested fees are allocated80/10/5/5 before burning residual principal. Requires unchanged main owner/key/ticks/L, bucket fields except idle, Registry/policy controls and governance/pause through callbacks. Desired mode remains unchanged; setMode(false) first to keep future adjustments single. Mixed residuals are accepted. Failure/minima/late callback rolls back all effects; no USD/swap/operations-enabled dependency or keeper automation. | Selected governance only; sealed/globally paused, operating canonical lifecycle without preparation or terminal LP exit; nonreentrant. | `r: (uint256,bytes32,uint256,uint256,uint256,uint64,uint64,address,address,uint128,uint128,uint256,bytes32,bytes32)` (struct ZZZMigration.ParkingRequest) — ParkingRequest(bucketId,poolId,mainLpId,totalShares,depositUSD,custodyEpoch,parkingNonce,token0,token1,minimum0,minimum1,deadline,reviewHash,portfolioHash). Exact current main/associated residual, shares and L-sensitive Manager.executionPortfolioFingerprint; token0 native ETH and token1 fixed ZZZ. Minima cover residual principal receipts ONLY (wei/raw ZZZ), at least one positive; deadline now..now+600; nonzero review commitment.<br>`r.bucketId: uint256`<br>`r.poolId: bytes32`<br>`r.lpTokenId: uint256`<br>`r.totalShares: uint256`<br>`r.depositUSD: uint256`<br>`r.custodyEpoch: uint64`<br>`r.nonce: uint64`<br>`r.token0: address`<br>`r.token1: address`<br>`r.minimum0: uint128`<br>`r.minimum1: uint128`<br>`r.deadline: uint256`<br>`r.reviewHash: bytes32`<br>`r.portfolioHash: bytes32` | None in ABI. |
| `configureZZZ(address,bytes32,bytes32)`<br>nonpayable | Bind the fixed ZZZ token once before sealing, matching source admission, Rewards admission and immutable Buyback.<br>No post-seal token replacement or external beneficiary is configured. No Oracle price call is required by this identity binding. | Only the current bootstrap before sealing; unavailable after sealing. A distinct governance wallet is not the bootstrap. Additional guards: nonReentrant, unset fixed ZZZ identity matching source admission, Rewards and immutable Buyback. | `token: address` — Nonzero ZZZ ERC20 with code, exactly matching contribution source and Buyback.zzz.<br>`codeHash: bytes32` — Exact reviewed token runtime-code hash.<br>`reviewHash: bytes32` — Nonzero review commitment. | None in ABI. |
| `distributePrincipal(uint256[])`<br>nonpayable | Attempt automatic principal pushes to current NFT owners in isolated bounded subcalls.<br>Caller cannot choose recipients. Captures current ProtocolPolicy.payoutGas once per batch(default180000,governed100000..1000000) for each isolated atomic ETH+ZZZ payment. Receiver failure emits PrincipalDeferred and preserves entitlement; low outer gas stops before another attempt. No automatic retry or expiry. | Anyone; Ready phase and canonical operating lifecycle, not preparing. Ordinary pause does not block payouts. | `ids: uint256[]` — 1..32 actual NFT IDs. Already-paid, unallocated and zero-output entries are skipped; duplicates cannot pay twice. | `paid: uint256` — Number of nonzero NFT allocations successfully paid by this call; a deferred/low-gas batch may pay fewer than requested. |
| `emergencyRebalance((uint256,bytes32,uint8,int24,int24,bytes32,bool,uint128,uint128,uint128,uint256,bytes32))`<br>nonpayable | Manually close/optionally swap/redeploy one shared LP while globally paused, under an exact custody-bound manifest.<br>Nonpayable; submitting governance wallet/Safe pays gas. Structural fixed assets/admitted venues/Vault custody/exact deltas/shares/reward reserves remain; per-call optional financial bypass is an explicit governance trust expansion. Full failure rolls back state; gas is spent. Event records retained mask and exact request/review commitment. Context is cleared on success; system remains paused. | Current governance only; sealed/paused, operating, no canonical preparation/import/retirement or terminal LP exit. Nonreentrant; not a keeper fallback. | `request: (uint256,bytes32,uint8,int24,int24,bytes32,bool,uint128,uint128,uint128,uint256,bytes32)` (struct EmergencyRebalance) — EmergencyRebalance in exact ABI order: bucket(shared custody ID); custodyHash(current chain/Vault/full Position/total-share review hash); checks(retained bits1=raw trigger/cooldown,2=price agreement,4=financial loss/idle/quality,8=operations flag,16=sampled confirmation;0..31); lower/upper(raw spaced physical ticks, lower&lt;upper); swapPool(admitted native ETH/ZZZ venue or zero for no swap); zeroForOne(true sells ETH); amountIn(exact raw swap input, zero=no swap); minimumOut(positive raw output if swapping, zero without swap); minimumLiquidity(positive raw LP floor); deadline(now..now+600); reviewHash(nonzero exact review commitment). No swap requires swapPool and minimumOut zero; amounts fit positive signed-int128 deltas. Disabled financial checks can lose almost all principal.<br>`request.bucket: uint256`<br>`request.custodyHash: bytes32`<br>`request.checks: uint8`<br>`request.lower: int24`<br>`request.upper: int24`<br>`request.swapPool: bytes32`<br>`request.zeroForOne: bool`<br>`request.amountIn: uint128`<br>`request.minimumOut: uint128`<br>`request.minimumLiquidity: uint128`<br>`request.deadline: uint256`<br>`request.reviewHash: bytes32` | None in ABI. |
| `invalidateParking(uint256)`<br>nonpayable | Advance the shared bucket nonce to invalidate unused parking and residual-close reviews.<br>Does not undo mined parking or residual closure, move principal, cancel collective exit or reopen retired LP. An already-broadcast operation still needs same-hash receipt reconciliation. | Drain-authorized governance before irreversible LP exit. | `id: uint256` — Stable shared bucket ID, not an individual NFT ID, mint ordinal or Uniswap LP token ID. | None in ABI. |
| `moveFunds(uint256,bytes32,uint128,uint256)`<br>nonpayable | Atomically move a live shared LP to an admitted native ETH/fixed-ZZZ pool with identical actual currencies.<br>Preserves NFT shares and captured maintenance budget; no external custodian or principal payout. A different fee tier is a different pool ID. | Current governance; live and before irreversible LP exit. | `id: uint256` — Stable shared bucket ID, not an individual NFT ID, mint ordinal or Uniswap LP token ID.<br>`pool: bytes32` — Exact initialized/admitted destination PoolKey hash, not a pool contract address.<br>`minimum: uint128` — Minimum actual replacement Uniswap liquidity units; not token units or USD.<br>`deadline: uint256` — Inclusive Unix timestamp in seconds, no earlier than this block and no more than ten minutes ahead. | None in ABI. |
| `park((uint256,bytes32,uint256,uint256,uint256,uint64,uint64,address,address,uint128,uint128,uint256,bytes32,bytes32))`<br>nonpayable | Harvest/close both exactly reviewed LP legs into Vault idle without releasing custody.<br>Custody4 appends portfolioHash; old custody3 calldata incompatible. Compounding can invalidate review. No USD-oracle prerequisite,external recipient or holder distribution. | Drain-authorized governance while globally paused,before terminal exit; nonreentrant. | `r: (uint256,bytes32,uint256,uint256,uint256,uint64,uint64,address,address,uint128,uint128,uint256,bytes32,bytes32)` (struct ZZZMigration.ParkingRequest) — ParkingRequest(bucketId,poolId,mainLpId,totalShares,depositUSD,custodyEpoch,nonce,token0,token1,minimum0,minimum1,deadline,reviewHash,portfolioHash). portfolioHash equals current Manager.executionPortfolioFingerprint(main), including BOTH L values. Minima are combined raw close proceeds; ETH uses wei; deadline fresh/reviewHash nonzero.<br>`r.bucketId: uint256`<br>`r.poolId: bytes32`<br>`r.lpTokenId: uint256`<br>`r.totalShares: uint256`<br>`r.depositUSD: uint256`<br>`r.custodyEpoch: uint64`<br>`r.nonce: uint64`<br>`r.token0: address`<br>`r.token1: address`<br>`r.minimum0: uint128`<br>`r.minimum1: uint128`<br>`r.deadline: uint256`<br>`r.reviewHash: bytes32`<br>`r.portfolioHash: bytes32` | None in ABI. |

## ZZZMigrationBudget

Standalone fixed-controller Vault companion for captured contribution/maintenance accounting. Aggregate both legs, freeze original-source/target controls, prove replacement utilization and retain exact reserve/custody separation.

[Source](../src/ZZZMigrationBudget.sol). Category: Project contract.

### Deployment

| Function / mutability | Purpose | Who / conditions | Inputs | Outputs |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `constructor(address)`<br>nonpayable | Bind controller without embedding Budget initcode in Vault. | Deployment only; canonical Vault parent resolves from controller module binding, not constructor caller. | `controller: address` — Fixed AccessController; actual Vault constructor later validates this Budget/controller/capability. | None in ABI. |

| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `DEFAULT_COMPOUND_INTERVAL()`<br>view | Read initial standalone compound interval before a per-pool governance override. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: uint32` — 3600seconds (one hour), an eligibility minimum rather than a mandatory hourly trade. |
| `EMERGENCY_REBALANCE_VERSION()`<br>view | Read manual paused-governance emergency integration with full current portfolio review identity. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: uint256` — 2; never an automatic keeper fallback or runtime upgrade. |
| `IDLE_COMPOUNDING_VERSION()`<br>view | Read independent compound interval and explicit replacement/compound context capability. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: uint256` — 4; historical versions retain separate clocks/contexts. |
| `MAX_LOSS_BPS()`<br>view | Read the immutable upper bound for the component’s governed execution-loss setting.<br>Returns: 1,000 basis points = 10%; not the current operating cap. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: uint16` — 1,000 basis points = 10%; not the current operating cap. |
| `REBALANCE_CONFIRMATION_VERSION()`<br>view | Read threshold/cooldown-separated sampled confirmation capability. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: uint256` — 3; historical outside-range confirmation remains separate. |

Only fixed Vault can execute the guarded checkpoint. | `id: uint256` — Canonical shared bucket ID, not an individual NFT or Uniswap position NFT ID. | `output0: bool` — True only for active physical LP with operations enabled, compoundInterval(pool) elapsed since max(lastCompound,lastRebalance), no raw needsRebalance candidate and no available preapproved range selection. |

| Function / mutability | Purpose | Who / conditions | Inputs | Outputs |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `beginEmergency(uint256,uint8)`<br>nonpayable | Arm a temporary bucket/governance-scoped emergency context. | Fixed Migration module only; sealed/paused/operating, no existing context/snapshot and no preparation/terminal exit. | `bucket: uint256` — Shared Vault custody bucket ID, not an individual NFT or physical LP token ID.<br>`checks: uint8` — Per-call retained-check bit mask0..31 validated by the fixed Migration entry; no persistent policy change. | None in ABI. |
| `checkpoint(uint256,uint8)`<br>nonpayable | Capture/extend/verify one atomic maintenance budget using fixed starting prices and stable governance/source-policy controls across the complete close/swap/deploy operation.<br>0 captures normal replacement;1 includes exact released compound;2 verifies/clears;3 starts standalone compound without hybrid permission. Freeze original source pool/raw observations and per-pool/trigger and scoped maintenance identity across callbacks; Manager separately freezes target controls. Same-pair moves compare ORIGINAL-source evidence, not a deliberately new pool hash/readiness. Aggregate both LPs at starting prices. Loss,captured governed idle and actual per-pool utilization are separate; utilization applies only to enabled normal replacement. No cumulative policy/withdrawal. | Only fixed Vault. | `id: uint256` — Canonical shared bucket ID, not an individual NFT or physical LP NFT ID.<br>`phase: uint8` — Internal maintenance checkpoint phase:0start,1compound,2verify,3idle-compoundstart. | None in ABI. |
| `contributionNav(uint256)`<br>nonpayable | Capture incumbent deployed/idle contribution NAV through fixed pricing context.<br>Not a public view: freezes the contribution basis used for fair share issuance. | Only fixed Vault. | `id: uint256` — Canonical shared bucket ID, not an individual NFT or physical LP NFT ID. | `deployed: uint256` — Captured incumbent deployed LP value in USD18.<br>`idle: uint256` — Captured attributed idle value in USD18. |
| `contributionResult(uint256,uint256,uint256,uint256,uint256,uint256,uint256,uint32)`<br>nonpayable | Verify captured marginal deployment/net backing and compute the contributor’s fair shares.<br>Excludes incumbent principal/idle/historical compound subsidies; closes the matching pricing context. Returns: Issued nonredeemable shares and verified netUSD in USD18. | Only fixed Vault. | `bucket: uint256` — Canonical shared bucket ID, not an NFT ordinal.<br>`inputUSD: uint256` — Captured original source value in USD18, not raw input units.<br>`supply: uint256` — Incumbent bucket’s nonredeemable share supply before this contribution.<br>`incumbentNAV: uint256` — Incumbent attributed LP/idle/backing value in USD18.<br>`incumbentDeployed: uint256` — Incumbent deployed physical LP value in USD18.<br>`deployed: uint256` — Measured physical LP value in USD18.<br>`idle: uint256` — Attributed idle value in USD18.<br>`limits: uint32` — Packed venue/contribution limits captured before callbacks; low16/high16 basis points. | `issued: uint256` — New nonredeemable shares issued at incumbent NAV.<br>`netUSD: uint256` — Verified net contribution value in USD18. |
| `endEmergency()`<br>nonpayable | Validate completion and clear all temporary emergency bucket/mask/governance state. | Fixed Migration module only, validated active context and no unfinished value snapshot. | None in ABI. | None in ABI. |
| `importComposition(uint256,uint64)`<br>nonpayable | Restore the source-authenticated composition epoch in an inert successor. | Only fixed VaultCanonical in importer state with unused epoch slot. | `id: uint256` — Canonical shared bucket ID, not an individual NFT or physical LP NFT ID.<br>`epoch: uint64` — Source-authenticated composition epoch, preserving backing identity. | None in ABI. |
| `recordComposition(uint256)`<br>nonpayable | Advance a bucket’s composition epoch after supported actual custody return.<br>Identifies backing composition; does not issue shares or value external assets. | Only fixed Vault. | `id: uint256` — Canonical shared bucket ID, not an individual NFT or physical LP NFT ID. | None in ABI. |
| `setCompoundInterval(bytes32,uint32)`<br>nonpayable | Configure a pool standalone-compound interval without changing backing or recorded clocks.<br>Emits CompoundIntervalChanged. A shorter interval may make work immediately eligible; it never resets lastCompound/lastRebalance or changes the pool rebalance interval. Existing local service intervals can impose additional delay. | Before sealing: only the current bootstrap address, not a distinct governance wallet. After sealing: only current governance with canonicalState=0 and no preparation. No waiting period; ordinary pause alone does not block configuration. Additional guards: nonReentrant, no preparation/canonical lock or active captured snapshot. | `poolId: bytes32` — Exact admitted pool ID whose ETH/ZZZ compound cadence will change.<br>`interval: uint32` — Seconds from600 through604800 (seven days). | None in ABI. |
| `setLossLimits(uint16,uint16)`<br>nonpayable | Set contribution and maintenance caps for future operations within hard 10% maxima.<br>Both values supplied together. Captured operation limits and original custody floors cannot be retroactively loosened; gas separate. Inputs: Basis points: default contribution200 and maintenance300. | Before sealing: only the current bootstrap address, not a distinct governance wallet. After sealing: only current governance with canonicalState=0 and no preparation. No waiting period; ordinary pause alone does not block configuration. Additional guards: nonReentrant, no preparation/canonical lock or active captured snapshot. | `contribution: uint16` — Future contribution-loss limit in basis points, at most1,000.<br>`maintenance: uint16` — Future maintenance-loss limit in basis points, at most1,000. | None in ABI. |
| `validateContribution(uint256,uint256,uint256,uint256,uint128,bool)`<br>nonpayable | Validate the NFT/bucket contribution and freeze price plus venue/contribution caps.<br>Delegates fixed ownership/custody checks, then starts the immutable pricing companion context. Returns: Ordered actual pair addresses plus packed uint32 limits: low16 venue, high16 captured contribution bps. | Only fixed Vault. | `id: uint256` — Canonical PoPL NFT ID, not mint ordinal or shared bucket ID.<br>`bucket: uint256` — Canonical shared bucket ID, not an NFT ordinal.<br>`inputUSD: uint256` — Captured original source value in USD18, not raw input units.<br>`inputTotal: uint256` — Packed original contribution amount identity used by current Pricing context; not USD.<br>`minimumLiquidity: uint128` — Minimum actual Uniswap liquidity units to create/add; not token units or USD.<br>`activation: bool` — True applies dormant activation rules; false applies existing-NFT top-up rules. | `token0: address` — Actual ordered currency0 address.<br>`token1: address` — Actual ordered currency1 address.<br>`limits: uint32` — Packed limits uint32: low16 venue bps, high16 captured contribution-loss bps. |

## ZZZNFT

ERC721 canonical ownership, fixed finite random artwork draw, persistent royalty eligibility and current-LP metadata.

[Source](../src/ZZZNFT.sol). Category: Project contract.

### Deployment

| Function / mutability | Purpose | Who / conditions | Inputs | Outputs |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `constructor(address,string)`<br>nonpayable | Deploy canonical ERC721 with immutable royalty ledger, claim router and metadata companions. | Deployment only; use the reviewed controller/royalty factory. | `controller: address` — Exact collection controller.<br>`baseURI: string` — Reviewed IPFS/Arweave artwork root; empty draft may be populated once before seal. A nonempty constructor root cannot later be switched. | None in ABI. |

## ZZZNFTCanonical

Seal-bound ownership/trait/inventory export/import codec; no fungible custody domain.

### Deployment

| Function / mutability | Purpose | Who / conditions | Inputs | Outputs |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `constructor(address,address)`<br>nonpayable | Deploy and bind Seal-bound ownership/trait/inventory export/import codec; no fungible custody domain. | Deployment only; required creator/controller/parent checks apply before this instance can be bound/sealed. | `controller: address` — Fixed AccessController address for this deployment’s roles and lifecycle.<br>`collection: address` — Existing NFT contract bound to the same controller; canonical codec does not create a second collection. | None in ABI. |

### Read functions

| Function / mutability | Purpose | Who / conditions | Inputs | Outputs |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `access()`<br>view | Identify the fixed controller that supplies this component’s module, governance and lifecycle authorization.<br>Reading the controller address grants no authority. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: address` (contract IZZZAccess) — The fixed controller that supplies this component’s module, governance and lifecycle authorization. |
| `canonicalAssetCount()`<br>pure | Report that the NFT ownership codec has no fungible backing legs.<br>Returns: Always zero; actual principal/rewards belong to other domains. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: uint256` — Always zero; actual principal/rewards belong to other domains. |
| `canonicalCount()`<br>view | Read the version-specific exported record count for NFT ownership, immutable traits and remaining artwork inventory.<br>Record rows are not necessarily NFTs; headers/assets/indices may add rows. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: uint256` — The version-specific exported record count for NFT ownership, immutable traits and remaining artwork inventory. |
| `canonicalExport(uint256)`<br>view | Encode one exact captured NFT ownership, immutable traits and remaining artwork inventory record.<br>Versioned ABI bytes consumed by authenticated import, not a general transaction. Inputs: Input is zero-based canonical record row, not necessarily an NFT ID. | Anyone; read-only. | `row: uint256` — Zero-based versioned canonical record row, not necessarily an NFT ordinal. | `output0: bytes` — Encode one exact captured NFT ownership, immutable traits and remaining artwork inventory record. |
| `canonicalValidate()`<br>view | Assert complete imported/restored record and backing consistency for NFT ownership, immutable traits and remaining artwork inventory.<br>Reverts on inconsistency; a successful call alone does not activate a successor or approve release. | Anyone; read-only assertion. | None in ABI. | None in ABI. |
| `nft()`<br>view | Identify the immutable NFT whose canonical ownership/trait records this codec serves. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: address` (contract IZZZNFTCanonicalState) — The immutable NFT whose canonical ownership/trait records this codec serves. |

| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `canonicalActivate()`<br>nonpayable | Activate the validated imported NFT ownership, immutable traits and remaining artwork inventory state.<br>Requires its authenticated funded cutover; cannot be used as a generic unpause. | Only fixed CanonicalHandoff. | None in ABI. | None in ABI. |
| `canonicalCapture()`<br>nonpayable | Capture the immutable cutoff for NFT ownership, immutable traits and remaining artwork inventory.<br>Requires the canonical freeze lifecycle; no ordinary admin snapshot/write. | Only fixed CanonicalHandoff. | None in ABI. | None in ABI. |
| `canonicalImport(uint256,bytes)`<br>nonpayable | Import the next source-authenticated NFT ownership, immutable traits and remaining artwork inventory record into an inert successor.<br>Enforces ordering, bounds and compatible schema; no arbitrary ledger rewrite. | Only fixed CanonicalHandoff. | `row: uint256` — Zero-based versioned canonical record row, not necessarily an NFT ordinal.<br>`record: bytes` — Exact versioned ABI-encoded canonical record authenticated against its source. | None in ABI. |
| `canonicalSettle()`<br>nonpayable | Complete ordered cutoff settlement for NFT ownership, immutable traits and remaining artwork inventory.<br>Some domains need no additional writes, but retain the same authenticated phase boundary. | Only fixed CanonicalHandoff. | None in ABI. | None in ABI. |

## ZZZOracle

Existing safeguard cannot later be removed via setSequencerFeed. | None in ABI. |

| `MAX_DEVIATION_BPS()`<br>view | Read the immutable 500-bps maximum source-disagreement setting. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: uint16` — 500bps(5%) hard maximum configurable two-source disagreement; selected tolerance is separately reviewed. |

| `activeSequencerPolicy()`<br>view | Read the active immutable sequencer-admission policy ID. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: bytes32` — Current uptime-policy ID; zero if none has been installed, not a healthy observation. |
| `contributionPricing()`<br>view | Read the immutable contribution price-freezing/execution-policy helper. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: address` (contract ZZZContributionPricing) — Immutable Oracle-created noncustodial accounting companion for frozen contribution vectors and maintenance valuation. |

Unknown IDs return zero fields, which do not establish admissibility or independence.<br>`output0.token: address`<br>`output0.primary: (address,bytes32,bytes32,uint32,uint8)` (struct ZZZOracle.SourcePolicy)<br>`output0.primary.source: address`<br>`output0.primary.expectedCodeHash: bytes32`<br>`output0.primary.independenceId: bytes32`<br>`output0.primary.maxAge: uint32`<br>`output0.primary.feedDecimals: uint8`<br>`output0.secondary: (address,bytes32,bytes32,uint32,uint8)` (struct ZZZOracle.SourcePolicy)<br>`output0.secondary.source: address`<br>`output0.secondary.expectedCodeHash: bytes32`<br>`output0.secondary.independenceId: bytes32`<br>`output0.secondary.maxAge: uint32`<br>`output0.secondary.feedDecimals: uint8`<br>`output0.maxDeviationBps: uint16`<br>`output0.assetDecimals: uint8`<br>`output0.reviewHash: bytes32` |
| `policyAt(uint256)`<br>view | Read a policy ID at a zero-based admission-history ordinal. | Anyone; read-only. | `ordinal: uint256` — Zero-based immutable dual-source admission-history index, less than policyCount(). | `output0: bytes32` — Dual-policy ID at a zero-based history index; out-of-bounds index reverts. |
| `policyCount()`<br>view | Read immutable dual-source history length. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: uint256` — Number of recorded dual USD policies, including superseded ones. |
| `price(address)`<br>view | Read the conservative lower USD price from current validated bounds. Same dual-source and sequencer checks as priceBounds; cannot return a stale fallback. | Anyone; read-only. | `token: address` — token zero means ETH. | `normalizedUSD: uint256` — normalizedUSD is USD18 per whole token. |
| `priceBounds(address)`<br>view | Read validated current two-source lower/upper USD prices and exact round/policy fingerprint. Any failure reverts; there is no resolution by averaging outside tolerance. | Anyone; read-only. | `token: address` — token is actual asset address, zero for ETH. | `lower: uint256` — Minimum of the two validated answers, USD18 per whole token.<br>`upper: uint256` — Maximum of the two validated answers, same USD18 units.<br>`fingerprint: bytes32` — Domain/chain/oracle/policy/sequencer-policy/full-round commitment; no stale/single-source fallback. |

Not a swap quote or execution guarantee; applying venue loss minimums and actual output checks is separate. | Anyone; read-only. | `input: address` — Actual input asset, zero for native ETH; its upper USD bound is used.<br>`output: address` — Actual output asset, zero for native ETH; its lower USD bound is used.<br>`amount: uint256` — Raw input-token units, or wei for ETH; result is raw output units, not USD or gas. | `output0: uint256` — Raw output reference; same-asset returns amount after validating input bounds. |
| `referenceX96(address,address)`<br>view | Compute an ordered-pair oracle sqrt-price reference for v4 math. Uses validated lower prices and actual token decimals; this is not a market spot read. | Anyone; read-only. | `token0: address` — First actual ordered accounting currency, zero for native ETH.<br>`token1: address` — Second actual ordered accounting currency; prices and token decimals determine the raw-unit sqrt ratio. | `output0: uint160` — uint160 sqrt(token1/token0 raw-unit ratio)*2**96; user-facing inverse ZZZ-price orientation is separate Strategy policy. |
| `sequencerFeed()`<br>view | Read the current sequencer uptime dependency; zero is the deployment’s explicit no-feed mode. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: address` — Configured uptime source address, zero when absent initially; an installed safeguard has no remove-to-zero setter. |
| `sequencerPolicies(bytes32)`<br>view | Read immutable sequencer source/runtime/review/bootstrap provenance. | Anyone; read-only. | `input0: bytes32` — Immutable sequencer-policy ID from activeSequencerPolicy() or sequencerPolicyAt(ordinal). | `source: address` — Recorded uptime source address; zero fields may be an unknown policy.<br>`expectedCodeHash: bytes32` — Recorded expected outer runtime hash; not proof against every proxy/upstream change.<br>`reviewHash: bytes32` — Recorded review commitment; not a qualification certificate.<br>`bootstrapAdmission: bool` — True when recorded through constructor bootstrap admission rather than an explicit governed replacement. |
| `sequencerPolicyAt(uint256)`<br>view | Read sequencer policy ID at a zero-based history ordinal. | Anyone; read-only. | `ordinal: uint256` — Zero-based sequencer-policy history index, less than sequencerPolicyCount(). | `output0: bytes32` — Uptime-policy ID at a zero-based append-only history index; bounds are checked by array access. |
| `sequencerPolicyCount()`<br>view | Read sequencer-policy history length. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: uint256` — Number of recorded uptime policies, including superseded ones. |
| `tokenDecimals(address)`<br>view | Read admitted asset decimals (18 for native ETH); no active policy reverts. | Anyone; read-only. | `token: address` — Actual admitted asset address; zero means native ETH with 18 decimals. | `output0: uint8` — Stored admitted raw asset precision0..18; native ETH is18. This getter rejects missing policy; priceBounds separately checks live ERC20 precision has not changed. |
| `value(address,uint256)`<br>view | Compute lower-bound USD value for a raw asset amount. Price failure propagates; stablecoins are not assumed pegged. | Anyone; read-only. | `token: address` — Actual admitted asset address; zero means native ETH.<br>`amount: uint256` — Raw amount in the token’s own decimals, or wei for ETH; valuation result is floor-rounded USD18. | `output0: uint256` — Floor(amount*lowerPrice/10**assetDecimals), in USD18. |

Both current positive fresh rounds, runtime/decimals, distinct source/independence IDs and agreement are checked. Two addresses or IDs do not prove independence. Bad/stale/divergent sources fail closed; no peg/pool-spot/single-source fallback. | Before sealing: only the current bootstrap address, not a distinct governance wallet. Independent upstream/admin/data-market review is external, not proved by different addresses.<br>`secondary.source: address`<br>`secondary.expectedCodeHash: bytes32`<br>`secondary.independenceId: bytes32`<br>`secondary.maxAge: uint32`<br>`secondary.feedDecimals: uint8`<br>`maxDeviationBps: uint16` — Agreement band in basis points using the lower-price denominator;0 exact agreement through500 hard maximum. Selected policy 300 = 3%.<br>`reviewHash: bytes32` — Nonzero actual full dual-source admission/review commitment. | `id: bytes32` — New append-only policy ID; update approved dependent API/route/inventory profiles after governance admission. |
