It changes target policy, not existing physical LP ticks. | Bound Strategy module only; keepers cannot call Registry directly. | `poolId: bytes32` — PoolKey-derived admitted pool ID.<br>`width: uint16` — Exactly 2500 or 5000 BPS (±25%/±50% in approved orientation); internal Strategy-selected target, not user-chosen width. | None in ABI. |
| `configureNative(((address,address,uint24,int24,address),bool,bool,uint16,uint16,uint16,uint16,uint32,uint256,uint256))`<br>nonpayable | Admit or reconfigure a reviewed static hookless native ETH/fixed-ZZZ pool, including compatible future fee tiers. Every funded bucket remains returnable to holders as actual ETH + ZZZ.<br>Only native ETH/fixed ZZZ is admitted. During collective Closing/Allocating this remains locked. After Migration.exitPhase()==Ready(3), governance may validate/re-enable or admit a swap venue only with depositsEnabled=false. Ready means principal allocated; holder payouts may remain. All existing native/runtime/feed/reward-asset/parameter checks remain. Venue admission neither spends reserves nor reopens mint, activation, growth or principal; lpExitStarted remains irreversible. Width must exceed retained trigger&gt;=100bps and max deviation; near-extreme tick-rounded ranges may be unrepresentable. | Before sealing: only the current bootstrap address, not a distinct governance wallet. After sealing: only current governance with canonicalState=0 and no preparation. No waiting period; ordinary pause alone does not block configuration. Additional guards: exact nativeConfiguration admission/terminal-exit guards and full venue validation. | `config: ((address,address,uint24,int24,address),bool,bool,uint16,uint16,uint16,uint16,uint32,uint256,uint256)` (struct PoolConfig) — key.currency0: Ordered lower-address accounting currency; address zero is native ETH.; key.currency1: Ordered higher-address currency; configureNative requires the fixed canonical ZZZ.; key.fee: Static LP fee in millionths, not percent or BPS; e.g. 10020 means 1.002% LP fee. Protocol fee is separate.; key.tickSpacing: Positive signed-int24 v4 tick spacing; must match the actual initialized pool.; key.hooks: Must be address zero for the currently supported hookless venues.; depositsEnabled: Desired contribution acceptance; only one pool may be true at a time. Must be false for post-exit Ready-phase reserve venues.; operationsEnabled: Desired ordinary maintenance/deployment acceptance; does not erase custody/history.; rangeBps: Price width in basis points; 100..9999, with orientation selected separately by Strategy.; triggerBps: Retained historical policy1 movement field, at least100 and below rangeBps; policy2 never uses it to authorize ordinary replacement.; maxSpotDeviationBps: Allowed spot-versus-oracle deviation in BPS, 1..500 and below rangeBps.; maxSlippageBps: Per-venue swap minimum guard in BPS, 1..1000 and sufficient for static LP fee. Separate from aggregate contribution/maintenance caps.; minRebalanceInterval: Cooldown seconds, 600..604800.; minDepositUSD: Pool minimum in USD18 (1 USD=10**18); actual input must satisfy both current ProtocolPolicy action minimum and this stronger pool minimum.; maxPositionUSD: Maximum cumulative admitted gross contribution per NFT in USD18, at least minDepositUSD; not current NAV or a total pool cap.<br>`config.key: (address,address,uint24,int24,address)` (struct PoolKey)<br>`config.key.currency0: address`<br>`config.key.currency1: address`<br>`config.key.fee: uint24`<br>`config.key.tickSpacing: int24`<br>`config.key.hooks: address`<br>`config.depositsEnabled: bool`<br>`config.operationsEnabled: bool`<br>`config.rangeBps: uint16`<br>`config.triggerBps: uint16`<br>`config.maxSpotDeviationBps: uint16`<br>`config.maxSlippageBps: uint16`<br>`config.minRebalanceInterval: uint32`<br>`config.minDepositUSD: uint256`<br>`config.maxPositionUSD: uint256` | `poolId: bytes32` — Actual PoolKey-derived poolId; eligible future fee tiers remain possible. |
| `disablePool(bytes32,bool,bool)`<br>nonpayable | Stop new deposits and/or ordinary operations on an admitted pool without querying external tokens, venue or prices. True means DISABLE, false leaves the existing state unchanged. At least one flag must be true. Disabling deposits clears this pool as contributionPool. Re-enable only through full configureNative validation; post-exit re-admission requires Ready and deposits disabled. | Before sealing: only the current bootstrap address, not a distinct governance wallet. After sealing: only current governance with canonicalState=0 and no preparation. No waiting period; ordinary pause alone does not block configuration. | `poolId: bytes32` — Admitted exact PoolKey hash.<br>`disableDeposits: bool` — True only disables; false retains current flag. True also clears this current contribution pool.<br>`disableOperations: bool` — True only disables ordinary operations; false retains current flag. At least one disable flag must be true. | None in ABI. |

## ZZZPortfolioPolicy

Fixed noncustodial desired single/hybrid mode,85%-edge trigger,per-pool main allocation/residual width/gap and scoped revisions. No setter moves backing; actual guarded placement applies changes.

[Source](../src/ZZZPortfolioPolicy.sol). Category: Project contract.

| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `constructor(address)`<br>nonpayable | Bind controller; initialize single mode, threshold8500 and effective half-side residual default5000. | Deployment only; parent later resolves through controller module binding, not constructor caller. | `controller: address` — Fixed collection AccessController with deployed code. | None in ABI. |

| `HARD_MAXIMUM_RESIDUAL_BPS()`<br>view | Read the permanent upper envelope for residual allocation. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: uint16` — 4000bps(40%); current maximumResidualBps(pool) may be stricter,never a required allocation. |
| `HARD_MINIMUM_MAIN_BPS()`<br>view | Read the permanent lower envelope for governed per-pool main allocation. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: uint16` — 6000bps(60%); read minimumMainBps(pool) for actual current policy. |
| `PORTFOLIO_VERSION()`<br>view | Read per-pool governed allocation/gap and scoped controls. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: uint256` — 2; explicit fresh runtime,never a relabel or upgrade of an old deployment. |

## ZZZPythPriceFeed

Optional immutable Pyth-to-AggregatorV3 reader; not a publisher, paid update service, feed admission or independence proof.

[Source](../src/ZZZPythPriceFeed.sol). Category: Project contract.

| Function / mutability | Purpose | Who / conditions | Inputs | Outputs |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `constructor(address,bytes32,bytes32,int32,uint32,uint16,bytes32)`<br>nonpayable | Deploy one immutable reader for an exact Pyth product and observation policy. | Anyone may deploy; deployment does not admit it to Oracle or qualify upstream proxy/independence. | `source: address` — Reviewed Pyth Core contract with code.<br>`codeHash: bytes32` — Exact nonzero Core runtime hash; does not identify a proxy implementation.<br>`id: bytes32` — Exact nonzero reviewed USD feed/product ID.<br>`exponent: int32` — Fixed expected Pyth exponent from -18 through 0; reader decimals=-exponent.<br>`age: uint32` — Maximum original publish age in seconds, 1..86400.<br>`confidenceBps: uint16` — Maximum confidence/positive-price ratio, 0..300 basis points.<br>`review: bytes32` — Nonzero review commitment; not a proof of an independent audit. | None in ABI. |

| `SOURCE_CALL_GAS()`<br>view | Read the 300,000-gas maximum for a bounded upstream Pyth read; not a flat service fee. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: uint256` — 300000 gas ceiling for the exact128-byte getPriceUnsafe response; no update transaction is submitted. |

| `maxObservationGap()`<br>view | Read the maximum permitted elapsed gap between recorded on-chain samples. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: uint32` — Seconds;0 stopped/unconfigured. Positive gap &lt;=duration/2. Selected600/300 has no extra grace; a confirmed gap greater than300 restarts. |
| `observationFingerprint(uint256,bytes32)`<br>view | Hash stable Registry/source/quality/confirmation controls together with the raw recorded observation tuple.<br>Includes raw identity/firstSeen/lastSeen/side. Same-pair movement compares captured ORIGINAL source PoolId,never deliberate new destination. | Anyone; read-only. | `bucket: uint256` — Shared Vault custody bucket ID, not an individual NFT or physical LP token ID.<br>`poolId: bytes32` — Admitted static hookless native ETH/fixed-ZZZ PoolId in canonical raw order. | `output0: bytes32` — Exact hash of controls,identity,firstSeen,lastSeen,side; deliberately not readiness against a newly replaced LP identity. |
| `observations(uint256)`<br>view | Read the last recorded sample history for a shared bucket, including stale or superseded records.<br>Stored values alone do not establish readiness; compare current condition, gap, policy and ready(bucket) at the same canonical block. | Anyone; read-only. | `input0: uint256` — Canonical shared bucket ID, not an individual collection NFT ID or physical Uniswap LP NFT ID. | `identity: bytes32` — Recorded stable condition hash; zero after reset.<br>`firstSeen: uint64` — First matching sample Unix seconds.<br>`lastSeen: uint64` — Latest matching sample Unix seconds.<br>`side: uint8` — Threshold side1 lower raw sqrt(ZZZ up),2 upper raw sqrt(ZZZ down);0 empty. Not actual-range-exit status. |
| `policyRevision()`<br>view | Read monotonic confirmation-policy revision, incremented by configure and Strategy invalidation. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: uint64` — uint64 revision; changed-and-restored settings cannot revive prior observations. |
| `ready(uint256)`<br>view | Check current raw condition and matching recorded evidence without writing or extending samples.<br>First enforce raw due condition/hard clock,then matching fresh threshold/reference evidence. Evidence may begin during cooldown but cannot bypass it. Deliberate geometry exception retains raw/financial guards. Service jitter separate; no inclusion guarantee. | Anyone; read-only. | `bucket: uint256` — Canonical shared bucket ID, not an individual collection NFT ID or physical Uniswap LP NFT ID. | `output0: bool` — True for a raw-guarded manual range exception, or positive configured gap with matching side/identity, elapsed duration and fresh last sample; otherwise false. |
| `referenceReviewHash()`<br>view | Read the governance commitment for the independent-reference policy. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: bytes32` — Zero at default true construction; changing/relaxing policy requires a nonzero commitment, not proof of review quality. |
| `requireReferenceExit()`<br>view | Read whether independent reference must corroborate the same edge-threshold side. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: bool` — Default true. False explicitly relaxes reference corroboration and invalidates old evidence; no price/output guard is disabled. |
| `reviewHash()`<br>view | Read current governance-provided confirmation-policy review commitment. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: bytes32` — bytes32 review hash; constructorzero means no reviewed configuration. Hash presence alone is not independent approval. |
| `strategy()`<br>view | Resolve sole Strategy parent from controller registry. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: address` — Canonical Strategy,zero before binding/fixed after seal; no parent setter. |

### Write functions

| Function / mutability | Purpose | Who / conditions | Inputs | Outputs |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `configure(uint32,uint32,bytes32)`<br>nonpayable | Configure the sampled window and maximum gap, advancing policy revision so earlier samples cannot authorize the new policy.<br>Advance revision and invalidate old samples. Changes no funds,LP,service cadence or hard cooldown; deliberate width/mode geometry exception retains its separate raw guards. | Before sealing: only the current bootstrap address, not a distinct governance wallet. After sealing: only current governance with canonicalState=0 and no preparation. No waiting period; ordinary pause alone does not block configuration. Additional guards: operating canonical lifecycle and no preparation, no terminal LP exit, nonReentrant. | `secondsRequired: uint32` — Required span60..604800seconds; current selected minimum600(10minutes).<br>`maximumGap: uint32` — 0 stops ordinary readiness, otherwise1..secondsRequired/2; selected300seconds, no automatic grace.<br>`reviewed: bytes32` — Nonzero review commitment for cadence,receipt latency and policy. | None in ABI. |
| `invalidatePolicy()`<br>nonpayable | Advance confirmation revision after a Strategy orientation change without selecting a new duration/gap; retain post-LP-exit orientation configuration used by remaining buybacks.<br>Emits ConfirmationInvalidated. Old stored samples remain visible but their identity cannot match the new revision; governance/keepers cannot call this entry directly. This grants no post-exit configure, observation, reset or LP operation. | Only controller-bound Strategy; operating canonical lifecycle. Pause/completed LP exit do not block revision-only invalidation. | None in ABI. | None in ABI. |
| `observe(uint256)`<br>nonpayable | Record threshold sample during/after cooldown; reset for changed side/identity,excessive gap,no candidate or caught fault.<br>Successful receipt advances evidence. Default minimum600seconds,reviewed maxgap300,source cadence240 with earlier known-readiness reads. Re-entry/gaps reset; not continuous history. No LP/reward/fee movement. | Authorized keeper; sealed/unpaused, operating canonical lifecycle and no principal exit; nonreentrant. | `bucket: uint256` — Canonical shared bucket ID, not an individual collection NFT ID or physical Uniswap LP NFT ID. | `output0: bool` — True only when also due after hard clock(or deliberate exception). False may still store a valid cooldown sample. |
| `resetObservation(uint256)`<br>nonpayable | Delete recorded bucket evidence after an observed incident so it cannot be extended on recovery.<br>Clear-only operation emits ObservationReset(reason5). Confirm its receipt before recording new evidence after an off-chain fault. Does not change policy, cooldown, LP, fees or rewards. | Authorized keeper; operating canonical lifecycle and no principal exit. Ordinary global pause is allowed; nonreentrant. | `bucket: uint256` — Canonical shared bucket ID, not an individual collection NFT ID or physical Uniswap LP NFT ID. | None in ABI. |
| `setRequireReferenceExit(bool,bytes32)`<br>nonpayable | Set reviewed independent-reference corroboration policy and monotonically invalidate old samples.<br>No financial guard is disabled. Deliberate governance width/mode changes retain their hard-clock exception. ABI keeps its historical Exit name although trigger is now inside actual range. | Before sealing: only the current bootstrap address, not a distinct governance wallet. After sealing: only current governance with canonicalState=0 and no preparation. No waiting period; ordinary pause alone does not block configuration. Additional guards: operating canonical lifecycle and no preparation, no terminal LP exit, nonReentrant. | `required: bool` — True requires physical/reference beyond the same85%-edge side and no active automatic width policy; false explicitly relaxes that corroboration.<br>`reviewed: bytes32` — Nonzero reviewed policy commitment. | None in ABI. |

## ZZZRewards

NFT-indexed funded personal LP and equal collective entitlements, native conversion credits and current-owner claims.

[Source](../src/ZZZRewards.sol). Category: Project contract.

### Deployment

| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `constructor(address)`<br>nonpayable | Deploy and bind NFT-indexed funded personal LP and equal collective entitlements, native conversion credits and current-owner claims. | Deployment only; required creator/controller/parent checks apply before this instance can be bound/sealed. | `controller: address` — Fixed AccessController address for this deployment’s roles and lifecycle. | None in ABI. |

| `SCALE()`<br>view | Read the fixed-point denominator used by this reward ledger’s cumulative indices and fractions.<br>Accounting precision, not an amount payable to a holder. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: uint256` — The fixed-point denominator used by this reward ledger’s cumulative indices and fractions. |

| `canonicalAssetCount()`<br>view | Count classified backing legs captured for funded personal/collective reward indices, balances and fractions. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: uint256` — Classified backing legs captured for funded personal/collective reward indices, balances and fractions. |

| `claimable(uint256,address)`<br>view | Compute current funded personal plus collective rewards, including unsettled index increments.<br>Prefer this complete balance over summing stored components; no new venue harvest occurs. In raw units of the specified currency. |

This is not a payout. ## ZZZRoyaltyConverter

Fixed royalties-ledger-only executor converting earned ERC20 balances into native ETH. Shares the abstract ZZZRewardConverter implementation, but has its own address, immutable ledger, route policies and storage; no shared custody or new authority.

[Source](../src/ZZZRoyaltyConverter.sol). Category: Project contract.

### Deployment

| Function / mutability | Purpose | Who / conditions | Inputs | Outputs |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `constructor(address,address,address)`<br>nonpayable | Deploy and bind Fixed royalty-ledger-only executor converting already earned ERC20 royalties into native ETH under reviewed routes. | Deployment only; required creator/controller/parent checks apply before this instance can be bound/sealed. | `controller: address` — Fixed AccessController address for this deployment’s roles and lifecycle.<br>`receiver: address` — Immutable royalty ledger alone allowed to call this converter.<br>`wrappedETH: address` — Optional immutable WETH address for explicit unwrap/v3 paths; zero is allowed for native-only deployment. | None in ABI. |

| Function / mutability | Purpose | Who / conditions | Inputs | Outputs |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |

| `access()`<br>view | Identify the fixed controller that supplies this component’s module, governance and lifecycle authorization.<br>Reading the controller address grants no authority. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: address` (contract IZZZAccess) — The fixed controller that supplies this component’s module, governance and lifecycle authorization. |

| `route(bytes32)`<br>view | Read the immutable v3 route descriptor and its runtime/review pins. | Anyone; read-only. | `id: bytes32` — Exact admitted conversion-route hash. | `output0: (address,address,address,address,uint24,bytes32,bytes32,bytes32,bytes32,bytes32)` (struct ZZZRewardConverter.RoutePolicy) — The immutable v3 route descriptor and its runtime/review pins.<br>`output0.source: address`<br>`output0.router: address`<br>`output0.factory: address`<br>`output0.pool: address`<br>`output0.fee: uint24`<br>`output0.sourceCodeHash: bytes32`<br>`output0.routerCodeHash: bytes32`<br>`output0.factoryCodeHash: bytes32`<br>`output0.poolCodeHash: bytes32`<br>`output0.reviewHash: bytes32` |

Separate rarity-weighted royalty cohorts, receipt accounting, currency-failure isolation and funded current-owner claims.

### Deployment

| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `constructor(address)`<br>nonpayable | Deploy and bind Separate rarity-weighted royalty cohorts, receipt accounting, currency-failure isolation and funded current-owner claims. | Deployment only; required creator/controller/parent checks apply before this instance can be bound/sealed. | `nftController: address` — Immutable NFT contract whose current owners/rarity rights this helper serves. | None in ABI. |

| `SCALE()`<br>view | Read the fixed-point denominator used by this reward ledger’s cumulative indices and fractions.<br>Accounting precision, not an amount payable to a holder. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: uint256` — The fixed-point denominator used by this reward ledger’s cumulative indices and fractions. |

| `converter()`<br>view | Locate the fixed royalty-only ETH executor, if bound before sealing. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: address` — The fixed royalty-only ETH executor, if bound before sealing. |

| `eligibleWeight(address)`<br>view | Read the sum of rarity weights enrolled for one royalty currency. | Anyone; read-only. | `input0: address` — Actual currency address; zero denotes native ETH in the native-aware ledger. | `output0: uint256` — The sum of rarity weights enrolled for one royalty currency. |

| `isAsset(address)`<br>view | Check whether a royalty currency is admitted to this receiver. | Anyone; read-only. | `input0: address` — Actual currency address; zero denotes native ETH in the native-aware ledger. | `output0: bool` — Whether a royalty currency is admitted to this receiver. |

| `orphaned(address)`<br>view | Read quarantined royalty funds, including prefunds, pre-admission balances and receipts with no eligible cohort. | Anyone; read-only. | `input0: address` — Actual currency address; zero denotes native ETH in the native-aware ledger. | `output0: uint256` — Quarantined royalty funds, including prefunds, pre-admission balances and receipts with no eligible cohort. |

| `weightOf(uint256)`<br>view | Read the economic royalty weight already activated for this NFT.<br>Returns: Zero before activation; revealed rarity trait itself is still 1/2/3 on the NFT metadata path. | Anyone; read-only. | `input0: uint256` — Canonical NFT ID, not mint ordinal or bucket ID. | `output0: uint256` — Zero before activation; revealed rarity trait itself is still 1/2/3 on the NFT metadata path. |

| `activate(uint256,uint256)`<br>nonpayable | Register the activated NFT’s fixed rarity weight and enroll it in healthy royalty currencies.<br>Weights are 1/2/3. Failed optional currencies retain their old cohort; activation can still succeed. | Only the immutable NFT contract in active economic state. | `id: uint256` — Canonical PoPL NFT ID, not mint ordinal or shared bucket ID.<br>`weight: uint256` — Fixed activated rarity weight1/2/3; not an LP share or admin-defined reward multiplier. | None in ABI. |

Taxes/rebases/freeze behavior require review. | Only immutable NFT via its governed asset-admission function. | `token: address` — Actual currency address; zero denotes native ETH only in native-aware paths. | None in ABI. |
| `bindCanonicalController(address)`<br>nonpayable | Atomically bind the fresh receiver to its factory’s controller.<br>A standalone receiver does not create protocol migration authority. | Creating factory only; one-time binding. | `controller: address` — Fixed AccessController address for this deployment’s roles and lifecycle. | None in ABI. |

A distinct governance wallet is not the bootstrap. Additional guards: economic canonical state0, nonReentrant, one-time unused converter slot and exact ledger/controller bindings. | `candidate: address` — Contract with the exact required immutable parent/controller/ledger binding. | None in ABI. |
| `canonicalAccept(uint256,address)`<br>payable | Accept and classify actual successor funding for rarity-weighted royalty cohorts, balances, fractions and quarantine.<br>**Native value:** Exactly zero, including native legs: the authenticated handoff transfers actual backing first, then calls this zero-value acknowledgment. Direct payable funding is rejected.<br>Exact asset/value and before/after accounting checks; no invented backing. | Only fixed CanonicalHandoff. | `i: uint256` — Zero-based captured backing leg index.<br>`input1: address` — Unused source/destination-address argument retained by the common canonical ABI; authority comes from the fixed Handoff caller. | None in ABI. |
| `canonicalActivate()`<br>nonpayable | Activate the validated imported rarity-weighted royalty cohorts, balances, fractions and quarantine state.<br>Requires its authenticated funded cutover; cannot be used as a generic unpause. | Only fixed CanonicalHandoff. | None in ABI. | None in ABI. |
| `canonicalCapture()`<br>nonpayable | Capture the immutable cutoff for rarity-weighted royalty cohorts, balances, fractions and quarantine.<br>Requires the canonical freeze lifecycle; no ordinary admin snapshot/write. | Only fixed CanonicalHandoff. | None in ABI. | None in ABI. |
| `canonicalImport(uint256,bytes)`<br>nonpayable | Import the next source-authenticated rarity-weighted royalty cohorts, balances, fractions and quarantine record into an inert successor.<br>Enforces ordering, bounds and compatible schema; no arbitrary ledger rewrite. | Only fixed CanonicalHandoff. | `row: uint256` — Zero-based versioned canonical record row, not necessarily an NFT ordinal.<br>`record: bytes` — Exact versioned ABI-encoded canonical record authenticated against its source. | None in ABI. |
| `canonicalRelease(uint256,address)`<br>nonpayable | Release one exact captured backing leg for rarity-weighted royalty cohorts, balances, fractions and quarantine.<br>Supports authenticated funding/abort legs; not an ordinary holder withdrawal or sweep. | Only fixed CanonicalHandoff. | `i: uint256` — Zero-based captured backing leg index.<br>`destination: address` — Exact module/backing destination selected by authenticated canonical logic. | None in ABI. |
| `canonicalRestore(uint256,address)`<br>payable | Restore an actual returned backing leg for rarity-weighted royalty cohorts, balances, fractions and quarantine after abort.<br>**Native value:** Exactly zero, including native legs: the authenticated handoff transfers actual backing first, then calls this zero-value acknowledgment. Direct payable funding is rejected.<br>Restoration is not new revenue or authority to reduce original liabilities. | Only fixed CanonicalHandoff. | `i: uint256` — Zero-based captured backing leg index.<br>`input1: address` — Unused source/destination-address argument retained by the common canonical ABI; authority comes from the fixed Handoff caller. | None in ABI. |
| `canonicalSettle()`<br>nonpayable | Complete ordered cutoff settlement for rarity-weighted royalty cohorts, balances, fractions and quarantine.<br>Some domains need no additional writes, but retain the same authenticated phase boundary. | Only fixed CanonicalHandoff. | None in ABI. | None in ABI. |
| `claim(uint256,address)`<br>nonpayable | Synchronize and pay one NFT’s funded royalty balance to its current owner.<br>Ordinary pause and collective LP exit do not revoke royalty rights. A failed currency or receiver can still make this claim revert; actual paid raw units are not new earnings. | Current NFT owner; active economic state. | `id: uint256` — Canonical PoPL NFT ID, not mint ordinal or shared bucket ID.<br>`asset: address` — Actual currency address; zero denotes native ETH in native-aware paths. | `amount: uint256` — Raw currency amount actually paid. |
| `claimFor(uint256,address,address)`<br>nonpayable | Forward a router-authorized royalty claim directly to the rechecked current NFT owner.<br>No arbitrary payout recipient and no transfer-operator claim privilege. | Only the NFT’s immutable ClaimRouter. | `id: uint256` — Canonical PoPL NFT ID, not mint ordinal or shared bucket ID.<br>`asset: address` — Actual currency address; zero denotes native ETH in native-aware paths.<br>`owner: address` — Current NFT owner to recheck; not an arbitrary payout recipient. | `amount: uint256` — Forward a router-authorized royalty claim directly to the rechecked current NFT owner. In raw units of the specified currency. |
| `convertBatch(address,uint256[],bytes32,uint256,uint256)`<br>nonpayable | Convert selected earned royalty balances in one swap and credit ETH to those same NFTs.<br>At most 32 sorted IDs; no royalty reallocation to current membership and no payout during conversion. Returns: Total native ETH credited in wei. | Authorized keeper, sealed canonical NFT and live economic/operation state. | `asset: address` — Actual currency address; zero denotes native ETH in native-aware paths.<br>`ids: uint256[]` — Strictly increasing NFT ID list, bounded to32; duplicate entries are rejected.<br>`routeId: bytes32` — Exact previously admitted route hash, with kind/enabled/dependency policy rechecked.<br>`minimumETH: uint256` — Minimum actual native ETH output in wei; stronger venue/Oracle policy also applies.<br>`deadline: uint256` — Unix timestamp in seconds; must satisfy this operation’s freshness/deadline guards. | `output: uint256` — Total native ETH credited in wei. |
| `forwardLate(address)`<br>nonpayable | Forward post-cutoff royalties from a retired receiver to its one immutable immediate successor.<br>Old captured liabilities are not forwarded twice. Returns: Raw late amount forwarded. | Permissionless after authenticated canonical retirement. | `asset: address` — Actual currency address; zero denotes native ETH in native-aware paths. | `amount: uint256` — Raw late amount forwarded. |
| `fund(address,uint256)`<br>nonpayable | Pull an exact ERC20 royalty donation and synchronize its actual receipt.<br>Plain transfers also work. Only actual funds can back liabilities; not an activation payment. | Permissionless funder in active economic state. | `asset: address` — Actual currency address; zero denotes native ETH in native-aware paths.<br>`amount: uint256` — Raw units of the actual specified currency; not its USD value. | None in ABI. |
| `sync(address)`<br>nonpayable | Recognize actual newly arrived royalty funds and advance that currency’s eligible-weight index.<br>Recovered optional assets first allocate unseen old receipts to the old cohort, then enroll waiting NFTs. Returns: Raw amount recognized, not an individual payout. | Permissionless in active economic state. | `asset: address` — Actual currency address; zero denotes native ETH in native-aware paths. | `amount: uint256` — Raw amount recognized, not an individual payout. |
| `syncAll()`<br>nonpayable | Synchronize every admitted royalty currency using normal accounting checks.<br>Unlike isolated activation, a failed asset can revert this all-assets operation. | Permissionless in active economic state. | None in ABI. | None in ABI. |
| `syncForActivation(address)`<br>nonpayable | Run one optional currency’s bounded synchronization inside activation’s isolated self-call.<br>External callers use sync(); failure cannot enroll newcomers into unseen old receipts. | Only the ledger itself. | `asset: address` — Actual currency address; zero denotes native ETH in native-aware paths. | None in ABI. |

| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |

Existing funds/entitlements are not swept. | None in ABI. |

Inputs: id is an NFT ID; amounts are ordered pair raw units, native value must match; positive minimumLiquidity and deadline required. Permanently rejected once collective LP exit starts, including after ordinary unpause. | Current NFT owner or the authenticated fixed Activation Entry; live/operating. | `id: uint256` — Canonical PoPL NFT ID, not mint ordinal or shared bucket ID.<br>`poolId: bytes32` — Exact admitted bytes32 hash of the v4 PoolKey, not a pool contract address.<br>`amount0: uint256` — Raw units of actual ordered currency0; native ETH uses wei.<br>`amount1: uint256` — Raw units of actual ordered currency1.<br>`minimumLiquidity: uint128` — Minimum actual MAIN physical liquidity units added/minted; not aggregate L,token units or USD.<br>`deadline: uint256` — Unix timestamp in seconds; must satisfy this operation’s freshness/deadline guards. | None in ABI. |

| `canonicalWrite(uint8,bytes)`<br>nonpayable | Apply one typed source-authenticated Vault accounting record to an inert successor.<br>Record kinds cover mint sequence, per-NFT rights, bucket state and aliases; not arbitrary storage writes. | Only fixed VaultCanonical during importer state. | `kind: uint8` — Explicit versioned record/proof type; unsupported values revert.<br>`row: bytes` — Zero-based versioned canonical record row, not necessarily an NFT ordinal. | None in ABI. |

| `rebalance(uint256,uint128,uint256)`<br>nonpayable | Replace complete shared portfolio after guarded edge-threshold/sample readiness and fee settlement.<br>Default85% of actual centre-to-boundary PRICE distance, not actual range exit. Hard governed cooldown and confirmation3 apply; deliberate geometry changes retain their raw-guarded exception. Off-chain jitter60..300seconds is separate. Both old legs close atomically. ## ZZZVaultCanonical

Immutable Vault companion for ordinary read-only owner/contribution/holder-exit validation and finite canonical export/import of captured backing. No configurable executor or delegatecall; typed validators cannot move custody.

[Source](../src/ZZZVaultCanonical.sol). Category: Project contract.

No state or custody changes. Requires paused terminal LP exit, Active/Parked custody and nonzero bucket shares; no USD price read. | Only this fixed Vault's Budget; read-only. | `id: uint256` — Shared Vault custody bucket ID, not a PoPL NFT ID or physical LP ID.<br>`migrationCaller: address` — Original Migration caller supplied by the module-only Vault, not a recipient or arbitrary user-selected wallet. | `token0: address` — Currency0: native ETH represented by address zero.<br>`token1: address` — Currency1: admitted fixed ZZZ, equal to the original Migration caller's currentZZZ(). |
| `validateMint(uint256,uint256,bytes32)`<br>view | Validate current mint price/policy on canonical home chain and derive identity plus captured recipient.<br>No OpenSea buyer/fee adapter,asset movement or identity reservation occurs in this view. | Only fixed Vault; read-only helper,not an operator mint entry point. | `value: uint256` — Actual native payment wei,must equal current mintPrice.<br>`ordinal: uint256` — Canonical mint ordinal for chain/origin-Vault token-ID derivation.<br>`expectedPolicy: bytes32` — Exact nonzero current combined mint fingerprint. | `output0: uint256` — Canonical uint256 NFT ID=keccak256(originChain,originVault,ordinal).<br>`output1: address` — Current accepted recipient for captured payment forwarding. |
| `validateOwner(uint256,address,bool,bool)`<br>view | Validate minted/home/reveal state and direct owner versus authenticated Entry caller.<br>Returns: Zero for direct owner or the matching fixed Entry address. | Only the actual fixed Vault migrationBudget helper; read-only. | `id: uint256` — Canonical PoPL NFT ID, not mint ordinal or shared bucket ID.<br>`caller: address` — Actual caller identity forwarded by the bound Vault/Budget; not arbitrary authorization.<br>`minted: bool` — Recorded mint-state input supplied by fixed Vault and checked against canonical ownership.<br>`activation: bool` — True applies dormant activation rules; false applies existing-NFT top-up rules. | `entry: address` — Zero for direct owner or the matching fixed Entry address. |
| `vault()`<br>view | Identify the immutable Vault whose source-authenticated records and exact backing this codec serves. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: address` (contract ICanonicalVaultState) — The immutable Vault whose source-authenticated records and exact backing this codec serves. |

Direct payable funding is rejected.<br>Exact asset/value and before/after accounting checks; no invented backing. | Only fixed CanonicalHandoff. | `index: uint256` — Zero-based captured/list index; it is not itself an asset address or NFT ID.<br>`input1: address` — Unused source/destination-address argument retained by the common canonical ABI; authority comes from the fixed Handoff caller. | None in ABI. |

Direct payable funding is rejected.<br>Restoration is not new revenue or authority to reduce original liabilities. | Only fixed CanonicalHandoff. | `index: uint256` — Zero-based captured/list index; it is not itself an asset address or NFT ID.<br>`input1: address` — Unused source/destination-address argument retained by the common canonical ABI; authority comes from the fixed Handoff caller. | None in ABI. |

<a id="maintenance"></a>

## Coverage and maintenance

The machine-readable [reference snapshot](function-reference.json) retains full ABI, canonical signatures, source hashes, reviewed explanations and uncovered explanations. [Explanation inputs](function-reference-notes.json) are the editable source. `python scripts/generate-function-reference.py --check` checks current source bindings and deterministic generated content without compiling or rerunning contract tests.

After a contract change, first obtain source-matching compiler input/output and normal size evidence, update the explanations, then regenerate. Unchanged source needs only the documentation check. A stale ABI, unknown note, duplicate signature, unexpected contract or altered generated file fails that check.

From the repository root, edit `docs/function-reference-notes.json`, then replace these example artifact paths with the reviewed matching files. Use `python3` if that is the Python command in your environment. This command imports existing compiler evidence; it does not compile or rerun contract tests.

```text
python scripts/generate-function-reference.py --compiler-input reviewed/compiler-input.json --compiler-output reviewed/compiler-output.json --size-report reviewed/size-gates.json
python scripts/generate-function-reference.py --check
```

6 current internal-only libraries have no callable ABI and are excluded: `ExactAsset`, `ExactToken`, `Ids`, `ZZZArtwork`, `ZZZPriceMath`, `ZZZV3VenueValidation`.
